  "short_description": {
    "id": "Succubus adalah iblis perempuan dalam cerita rakyat Eropa yang muncul dalam mimpi lelaki untuk merayunya, menguras kesehatannya, dan dapat melahirkan anak setengah iblis bernama cambion.",
    "en": "The succubus is a female demon of European folklore who visits men in dreams to seduce them, drains their health and may bear a half-demon child called a cambion.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Succubus, dari bahasa Latin succuba, \"yang berbaring di bawah\", adalah iblis perempuan yang muncul dalam mimpi lelaki untuk merayunya. Hubungan berulang dengannya dapat melahirkan cambion, anak setengah iblis, tetapi menguras jiwa dan raga lelaki itu hingga mati. Ia berwujud perempuan cantik yang bila diamati memiliki cakar burung atau ekor ular, dan kemudian juga digambarkan sebagai siren.",
      "en": "The succubus, from Latin succuba, 'one who lies beneath', is a female demon who appears in men's dreams to seduce them. Repeated unions can produce a cambion, a half-demon child, but drain the man body and mind until he dies. She looks like a beautiful woman who on closer view shows bird claws or a serpent's tail, and later lore also made her a siren.",
      "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Malleus Maleficarum (1486) menyebut succubi mengumpulkan air mani yang lalu dipakai incubi untuk menghamili perempuan, sementara Thomas Aquinas menganggap keduanya satu makhluk yang berganti wujud; Raja James membantah bahwa makhluk semacam itu dapat berkembang biak, dan Sinistrari menganggap mereka makhluk berakal yang bukan iblis. Merlin konon anak incubus. Imam dan rabi seperti Hanina ben Dosa berusaha membatasi kuasanya.",
      "en": "The Malleus Maleficarum (1486) says succubi gather semen that incubi then use to impregnate women, while Thomas Aquinas saw them as one shape-changing being; King James denied such beings could breed, and Sinistrari held them rational creatures, not demons.     {
      "id": "Kepercayaan ini didorong oleh mimpi basah dan kelumpuhan tidur. Kini succubus lebih sering digambarkan sebagai penggoda cantik daripada iblis menakutkan, dan menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia sasarannya adalah orang yang lemah iman atau kesepian.",
      "en": "The belief was driven by wet dreams and sleep paralysis. Today the succubus is more often drawn as a beautiful seductress than a frightening demon, and the Indonesian article names her targets as the weak in faith or the lonely.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "succubus-c01",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "A succubus ... succubi) is a female demon who was described in various European folklore as appearing in the dreams of male humans in order to seduce them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Succubus (jamak: succubi) adalah iblis perempuan yang dalam berbagai cerita rakyat Eropa digambarkan muncul dalam mimpi lelaki untuk merayu mereka.",
        "en": "A succubus (plural succubi) is a female demon described in various European folklore as appearing in men's dreams to seduce them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c02",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "Repeated interactions between a succubus and a man will lead to sexual activity. The establishment and perpetuation of such a relationship enables the production of a hybrid child known as a cambion, but at the expense of the man, whose mental and physical health will deteriorate rapidly, eventually resulting in his death if the succubus continues courting him for a protracted period.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hubungan berulang dengan succubus berujung pada persetubuhan dan dapat melahirkan anak hibrida yang disebut cambion, tetapi kesehatan jiwa dan raga lelaki itu cepat memburuk hingga ia mati bila succubus terus mendekatinya lama.",
        "en": "Repeated encounters lead to sex and can produce a hybrid child called a cambion, but at the cost of the man's mental and physical health, which declines fast until he dies if the succubus keeps courting him."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c03",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "In modern representations, a succubus is often depicted as a beautiful woman seductress or charming, rather than as demonic or frightening, to attract people instead of repulsing them. The male counterpart of the succubus is the incubus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam gambaran modern, succubus sering tampil sebagai perempuan cantik penggoda, bukan iblis menakutkan; padanan lelakinya adalah incubus.",
        "en": "In modern depictions the succubus is often a beautiful seductress rather than a frightening demon; her male counterpart is the incubus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c04",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "Historically, folkloric belief in succubi was motivated by distressing nighttime phenomena, chiefly wet dreams and sleep paralysis.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Secara historis, kepercayaan pada succubi didorong gejala malam yang meresahkan, terutama mimpi basah dan kelumpuhan tidur.",
        "en": "Historically, belief in succubi was driven by distressing night phenomena, chiefly wet dreams and sleep paralysis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c05",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "In medieval Europe, union of an incubus or succubus and a human was supposed by some to result in the birth of beings half-demon and half-human children. Legendary magician Merlin was said to have been fathered by an incubus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Eropa abad pertengahan, persatuan incubus atau succubus dengan manusia dianggap melahirkan anak setengah iblis setengah manusia; penyihir legendaris Merlin konon anak incubus.",
        "en": "In medieval Europe a union of an incubus or succubus with a human was thought by some to produce half-demon, half-human children; the legendary magician Merlin was said to be fathered by an incubus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c06",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "The term derives from Late Latin succuba \"paramour\" from succubare \"to lie beneath\" (sub- \"under\" and cubare \"to lie\"), used to describe this being's implied sexual position relative to the sleeper's position. The English word \"succubus\" dates from the late 14th century.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Istilah ini dari bahasa Latin Akhir succuba, \"kekasih gelap\", dari succubare, \"berbaring di bawah\" (sub-, \"di bawah\", dan cubare, \"berbaring\"); kata Inggris succubus muncul pada akhir abad ke-14.",
        "en": "The term comes from Late Latin succuba, 'paramour', from succubare, 'to lie beneath' (sub-, 'under', and cubare, 'to lie'); the English word dates from the late 14th century."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c07",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "A succubus may take the form of a beautiful woman, but closer inspection may reveal deformities of her body, such as bird-like claws or serpentine tails.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Succubus dapat berwujud perempuan cantik, tetapi bila diamati lebih dekat tampak cacat tubuhnya seperti cakar burung atau ekor ular.",
        "en": "A succubus may look like a beautiful woman, but a closer look may reveal deformities such as bird-like claws or a serpent's tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c08",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "In later folklore, a succubus took the form of a siren.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam cerita rakyat yang lebih kemudian, succubus berwujud siren.",
        "en": "In later folklore the succubus took the form of a siren."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c09",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "Throughout history, priests and rabbis, including Hanina ben Dosa and Abaye, tried to curb the power of succubi over humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sepanjang sejarah, imam dan rabi, termasuk Hanina ben Dosa dan Abaye, berusaha membatasi kuasa succubi atas manusia.",
        "en": "Through history priests and rabbis, including Hanina ben Dosa and Abaye, tried to curb the succubi's power over people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c10",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "According to the Malleus Maleficarum, or Witches' Hammer, written by Heinrich Kramer (Institor) in 1486, succubi collect semen from men they seduce. Incubi, or male demons, then use the semen to impregnate human females, thus explaining how demons could apparently sire children",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Malleus Maleficarum karya Heinrich Kramer (1486), succubi mengumpulkan air mani dari lelaki yang dirayunya, lalu incubi memakainya untuk menghamili perempuan, yang menjelaskan bagaimana iblis seolah dapat beranak.",
        "en": "According to the Malleus Maleficarum by Heinrich Kramer (1486), succubi collect semen from the men they seduce, and incubi then use it to impregnate women, explaining how demons could seemingly father children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c11",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "Children so begotten—cambions—were supposed to be those that were born deformed, or more susceptible to supernatural influences.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak-anak hasilnya, cambion, dianggap lahir cacat atau lebih rentan terhadap pengaruh gaib.",
        "en": "Children so begotten, cambions, were thought to be born deformed or more open to supernatural influence."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c12",
      "source_id": "succubus-s1",
      "quote": "King James in his dissertation titled Dæmonologie refutes the possibility for angelic entities to reproduce",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Raja James dalam risalahnya Dæmonologie membantah kemungkinan makhluk malaikat dapat berkembang biak.",
        "en": "King James in his treatise Dæmonologie rejects the idea that angelic beings can reproduce."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c13",
      "source_id": "succubus-s2",
      "quote": "Sasaran dari target incubus dan succubus adalah orang yang tidak kuat iman, tidak bisa menahan syahwat, orang yang tidak setia kepada pasangannya, atau orang yang kesepian.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia, sasaran incubus dan succubus adalah orang yang lemah iman, tak dapat menahan syahwat, tidak setia kepada pasangannya, atau kesepian.",
        "en": "According to the Indonesian article, the targets of incubi and succubi are people weak in faith, unable to restrain lust, unfaithful to their partners, or lonely."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c14",
      "source_id": "succubus-s2",
      "quote": "Cambion dikatakan akan tumbuh seperti anak-anak normal di usia sebelum 7 tahun",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cambion dikatakan tumbuh seperti anak biasa sebelum berusia 7 tahun.",
        "en": "A cambion is said to grow like an ordinary child before the age of 7."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c15",
      "source_id": "succubus-s3",
      "quote": "17世紀イタリアの神学者ルドヴィコ・マリア・シニストラリ（1622年 – 1701年）は著書にて、「インキュバス・サキュバスは悪魔ではなく、人間とは別の種類の理性的な動物」としている。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, teolog Italia abad ke-17 Ludovico Maria Sinistrari (1622–1701) menulis bahwa incubus dan succubus bukan iblis, melainkan makhluk berakal jenis lain di luar manusia.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, the 17th-century Italian theologian Ludovico Maria Sinistrari (1622–1701) wrote that incubi and succubi are not demons but a different kind of rational creature from humans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "succubus-c16",
      "source_id": "succubus-s3",
      "quote": "男性形のインキュバスとは表裏一体で、サキュバスの姿で男性から精液を採取し、それをインキュバスに変身後に女性へと注いで望まぬ妊娠をさせる存在とトマス・アクィナスは『神学大全』で記述しており、両性具有の同一者であるとも言われている。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "religious-tradition",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Thomas Aquinas dalam Summa Theologiae menggambarkannya sebagai makhluk yang mengambil air mani dari lelaki dalam wujud succubus lalu berubah menjadi incubus untuk menghamili perempuan, sehingga keduanya kadang dianggap satu makhluk berkelamin ganda.",
        "en": "Thomas Aquinas in the Summa Theologiae describes it as a being that takes semen from men as a succubus and then, turned incubus, impregnates women, so the two are sometimes said to be one hermaphrodite being."
      }
    }
  ],

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Binatang Gévaudan adalah satu atau lebih binatang pemangsa manusia yang meneror Gévaudan, Prancis, pada 1764–1767, yang identitasnya masih diperdebatkan sampai kini.",
    "en": "The Beast of Gévaudan was one or more man-eating animals that terrorised Gévaudan, France, in 1764–1767, whose identity is still debated.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "beast-of-gevaudan-c01",
      "beast-of-gevaudan-c04"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Binatang Gévaudan adalah nama untuk satu atau lebih pemangsa manusia yang meneror Gévaudan di Pegunungan Margeride, Prancis, sejak 30 Juni 1764; korban resmi pertamanya Jeanne Boulet, 14 tahun. Saksi menggambarkannya cokelat kemerahan bergaris gelap, sebesar anak sapi, berekor panjang berjumbai, dan bergerak seolah terbang; artikel bahasa Jepang menyebut ia sering menyasar kepala korban. Sebuah kajian tahun 1987 menghitung 210 serangan dan 113 korban tewas.",
      "en": "The Beast of Gévaudan is the name for one or more man-eaters that terrorised Gévaudan in the Margeride Mountains of France from 30 June 1764; its first official victim was 14-year-old Jeanne Boulet. Witnesses described it as russet with dark stripes, calf-sized, with a long tufted tail, moving as if flying; the Japanese article says it often went for the head. A 1987 study counted 210 attacks and 113 deaths.",

        "beast-of-gevaudan-c01",

      "id": "Kerajaan Prancis mengerahkan bangsawan, tentara, dan pemburu kerajaan untuk memburunya, dan beberapa binatang dibunuh sebelum serangan berhenti, meski saksi menyebut ia berkali-kali terluka tanpa mati. Identitasnya, dubuk, serigala, anjing besar, atau anjing-serigala, masih diperdebatkan, dan sumber berbeda soal akhir masa serangan (1767 atau 1770).",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c01",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "The Beast of Gévaudan ... is the historic name associated with a man-eating cryptid or cryptids that terrorized the former province of Gévaudan ... in the Margeride Mountains of south-central France between 30 June 1764 and 19 June 1767.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Binatang Gévaudan (La Bête du Gévaudan) adalah nama historis untuk satu atau lebih kriptid pemangsa manusia yang meneror bekas provinsi Gévaudan di Pegunungan Margeride, Prancis selatan-tengah, antara 30 Juni 1764 dan 19 Juni 1767.",
        "en": "The Beast of Gévaudan (La Bête du Gévaudan) is the historic name for one or more man-eating cryptids that terrorised the former province of Gévaudan in the Margeride Mountains of south-central France between 30 June 1764 and 19 June 1767."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c02",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "were said to have been committed by one or more beasts of a tawny/russet colour with dark streaks/stripes and a dark stripe down its back, a tail \"longer than a wolf's\" ending in a tuft according to contemporary eyewitnesses.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut saksi mata masa itu, serangan dilakukan satu atau lebih binatang berwarna cokelat kemerahan dengan belang gelap dan garis gelap di punggung, berekor lebih panjang daripada serigala dengan jumbai di ujungnya.",
        "en": "Contemporary witnesses said the attacks were by one or more tawny-russet beasts with dark stripes and a dark stripe down the back, with a tail 'longer than a wolf's' ending in a tuft."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c03",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "It was said to attack with formidable teeth and claws, and appeared to be the size of a calf or cow and seemed to fly or bound across fields towards its victims.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menyerang dengan gigi dan cakar yang mengerikan, tampak sebesar anak sapi atau sapi, dan seolah terbang atau melompat melintasi ladang ke arah korbannya.",
        "en": "It attacked with fearsome teeth and claws, looked the size of a calf or cow, and seemed to fly or bound across fields at its victims."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c04",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "These descriptions from the period could identify the beast as a striped hyena, a large wolf, a large dog, or a wolfdog, though its identity is still the subject of debate and remains unsolved to this day.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Gambaran itu dapat cocok dengan dubuk belang, serigala besar, anjing besar, atau anjing-serigala, tetapi identitasnya masih diperdebatkan sampai kini.",
        "en": "These descriptions could fit a striped hyena, a large wolf, a large dog or a wolfdog, but its identity is still debated and unsolved."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c05",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "The Kingdom of France used a considerable amount of wealth and manpower to hunt the animals responsible, including the resources of several nobles, soldiers, royal huntsmen, and civilians.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kerajaan Prancis mengerahkan banyak dana dan tenaga untuk memburunya, termasuk bangsawan, tentara, pemburu kerajaan, dan warga.",
        "en": "The Kingdom of France spent great wealth and manpower hunting the animals, including nobles, soldiers, royal huntsmen and civilians."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c06",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "A 1987 study estimated there had been 210 attacks, resulting in 113 deaths and 49 injuries; 98 of the victims killed were partly eaten.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah kajian tahun 1987 memperkirakan 210 serangan dengan 113 korban tewas dan 49 terluka; 98 korban tewas sebagian dimakan.",
        "en": "A 1987 study estimated 210 attacks causing 113 deaths and 49 injuries; 98 of those killed were partly eaten."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c07",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "Shortly afterwards, on 30 June, the beast's first official victim was recorded: 14-year-old Jeanne Boulet was killed near the village of Les Hubacs near Langogne.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Korban resmi pertamanya tercatat pada 30 Juni 1764: Jeanne Boulet, 14 tahun, yang tewas dekat desa Les Hubacs di dekat Langogne.",
        "en": "Its first official victim was recorded on 30 June 1764: 14-year-old Jeanne Boulet, killed near the village of Les Hubacs near Langogne."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c08",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s1",
      "quote": "Several animals identified as the beast were reportedly killed before the attacks finally stopped.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Beberapa binatang yang dianggap sebagai Binatang Gévaudan dilaporkan dibunuh sebelum serangan akhirnya berhenti.",
        "en": "Several animals identified as the beast were reportedly killed before the attacks finally stopped."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c09",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s2",
      "quote": "adalah nama historis yang merujuk kepada hewan mirip serigala pemangsa manusia yang meneror bekas provinsi Gévaudan (kini département Lozère bagian dari wilayah Haute-Loire), di Pegunungan Margeride di Prancis Selatan antara tahun 1764 dan 1770.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Indonesia menyebut serangan hewan mirip serigala pemangsa manusia ini berlangsung antara 1764 dan 1770.",
        "en": "The Indonesian article dates the man-eating wolf-like beast's attacks between 1764 and 1770."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c10",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s2",
      "quote": "Saksi juga menyatakan bahwa makhluk tersebut telah dilukai berkali-kali tetapi tidak kunjung mati.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Saksi juga menyatakan makhluk itu sudah dilukai berkali-kali, termasuk ditembak dari jarak dekat, tetapi tidak juga mati.",
        "en": "Witnesses also said the creature had been wounded many times, even shot at close range, yet did not die."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-gevaudan-c11",
      "source_id": "beast-of-gevaudan-s3",
      "quote": "この生き物の伝えられた殺害の仕方は捕食動物としては異常で、しばしば獲物の頭部を標的にし、普通なら捕食動物が狙う脚や喉を全く無視していた。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, cara membunuhnya tidak lazim bagi pemangsa: ia sering menyasar kepala korban dan mengabaikan kaki dan leher yang biasa disasar pemangsa.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article its killing method was unusual for a predator: it often went for the victim's head, ignoring the legs and throat predators usually target."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Incubus adalah iblis lelaki dalam cerita rakyat yang muncul dalam mimpi perempuan untuk merayunya, menguras kesehatannya, dan konon menjadi ayah Merlin.",
    "en": "The incubus is a male demon of folklore who appears in women's dreams to seduce them, drains their health and is said to have fathered Merlin.",

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Incubus, dari bahasa Latin incubō, \"yang menindih orang tidur\", adalah iblis lelaki yang muncul dalam mimpi perempuan untuk merayunya; hubungan berulang dengannya dapat melahirkan kambion tetapi merusak kesehatan bahkan membunuh. Ia konon dapat menyusup sebagai kelelawar, dan penyihir Merlin dikatakan anaknya. Kepercayaan ini didorong mimpi basah dan kelumpuhan tidur; kini ia sering digambarkan sebagai lelaki tampan.",
      "en": "The incubus, from Latin incubō, 'what lies on one while asleep', is a male demon who appears in women's dreams to seduce them; repeated unions can produce a cambion but ruin health or kill. He is said to slip in as a bat, and the magician Merlin was called his son. The belief was driven by wet dreams and sleep paralysis; today he is often drawn as a handsome man.",

      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam gambaran modern, incubus sering tampil sebagai lelaki tampan penggoda, bukan iblis menakutkan; padanan perempuannya adalah succubus.",
        "en": "In modern depictions the incubus is often a handsome seducer rather than a frightening demon; his female counterpart is the succubus."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c04",
      "source_id": "incubus-s1",
      "quote": "Historically, folkloric belief in incubi was motivated by distressing nighttime phenomena, chiefly wet dreams and sleep paralysis.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kepercayaan pada incubi didorong gejala malam yang meresahkan, terutama mimpi basah dan kelumpuhan tidur.",
        "en": "Belief in incubi was driven by distressing night phenomena, chiefly wet dreams and sleep paralysis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c05",
      "source_id": "incubus-s1",
      "quote": "Legendary magician Merlin was said to have been fathered by an incubus. Walter Stephens writes in his book Demon Lovers that some traditions hold that repeated sexual activity with an incubus or succubus may result in the deterioration of health, an impaired mental state, or even death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyihir legendaris Merlin konon anak incubus, dan Walter Stephens dalam Demon Lovers menulis bahwa menurut sebagian tradisi hubungan berulang dengan incubus atau succubus dapat merusak kesehatan, jiwa, bahkan menyebabkan kematian.",
        "en": "The legendary magician Merlin was said to be an incubus's son, and Walter Stephens writes in Demon Lovers that some traditions hold repeated relations with an incubus or succubus may ruin health and mind or even kill."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c06",
      "source_id": "incubus-s1",
      "quote": "The Late Latin word incubus (\"a nightmare induced by a demon\") is derived from Latin incubō (\"nightmare, what lies down on one whilst one sleeps\") and further from incubāre (\"to lie upon, to hatch\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata Latin Akhir incubus, \"mimpi buruk yang ditimbulkan iblis\", berasal dari incubō, \"yang menindih orang tidur\", dan incubāre, \"berbaring di atas\".",
        "en": "The Late Latin incubus, 'a demon-induced nightmare', comes from incubō, 'what lies on one while asleep', and incubāre, 'to lie upon'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c07",
      "source_id": "incubus-s1",
      "quote": "One of the earliest evident mentions of a demon sharing qualities with an incubus comes from Mesopotamia on the Sumerian King List, circa 2400 BC, where the hero Gilgamesh's father is listed as Lilu. Lilu is described as \"disturbing\" and \"seducing\" women in their sleep",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu sebutan tertua iblis mirip incubus ada dalam Daftar Raja Sumeria dari Mesopotamia sekitar 2400 SM, yang mencantumkan Lilu, iblis yang mengganggu dan merayu perempuan dalam tidur, sebagai ayah pahlawan Gilgamesh.",
        "en": "One of the earliest mentions of an incubus-like demon is on the Sumerian King List from Mesopotamia, c. 2400 BC, naming Lilu, who 'disturbs' and 'seduces' sleeping women, as the father of the hero Gilgamesh."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c08",
      "source_id": "incubus-s1",
      "quote": "Two other corresponding demons also appear in Mesopotamian accounts: Ardat lili, who visits men by night, and Idlu lili, a male counterpart to Ardat lili who visits women by night and begets from them.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Catatan Mesopotamia juga menyebut Ardat lili yang mendatangi lelaki pada malam hari dan Idlu lili, padanan lelakinya, yang mendatangi perempuan dan membuat mereka hamil.",
        "en": "Mesopotamian accounts also name Ardat lili, who visits men by night, and Idlu lili, her male counterpart, who visits women by night and begets on them."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c09",
      "source_id": "incubus-s3",
      "quote": "古くはアウグスティヌス『神の国』第15巻23章に、森の精霊シルウァーヌスやファウヌスはインキュバスと呼ばれ、しばしば女性を襲って欲望を満たす、という当時の俗信についての記述がある。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, Agustinus dalam De Civitate Dei buku 15 bab 23 mencatat kepercayaan masa itu bahwa roh hutan Silvanus dan Faunus disebut incubi dan sering menyerang perempuan.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, Augustine in The City of God (book 15, chapter 23) records the belief of his time that the forest spirits Silvanus and Faunus were called incubi and often assailed women."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "incubus-c10",
      "source_id": "incubus-s3",
      "quote": "どちらも、自分と性交したくてたまらなくさせるために、襲われる人の理想の異性像で、服を着ず下半身は裸で現れる。そのため、その誘惑を拒否することは非常に困難だった。人の形態をとるだけでなく、標的となった人間の寝室には蝙蝠に化けて侵入するともされている。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Incubus dan succubus muncul sebagai sosok lawan jenis idaman korbannya sehingga godaannya sangat sulit ditolak, dan selain berwujud manusia mereka konon menyusup ke kamar korban dalam wujud kelelawar.",
        "en": "Incubi and succubi appear as their victim's ideal partner, so their temptation is very hard to refuse, and besides human form they are said to slip into the victim's bedroom as bats."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en, id, dan ja; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."
    },

    },

    }
  ]
}

    "id": "Ogre adalah raksasa buruk rupa pemakan manusia, terutama anak-anak, dari dongeng Eropa, seperti dalam Puss in Boots dan Hop-o'-My-Thumb.",
    "en": "The ogre is a hideous man-eating giant, especially of children, from fairy tales such as Puss in Boots and Hop-o'-My-Thumb.",

      "id": "Ogre atau ogress adalah monster legendaris berwujud mirip manusia yang besar dan buruk rupa yang memakan manusia, terutama bayi dan anak-anak.       "en": "The ogre or ogress is a legendary monster, a large, hideous humanoid that eats people, especially babies and children.     {
      "id": "ogre-c01",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "An ogre (feminine: ogress) is a legendary monster depicted as a large, hideous, humanoid being that eats ordinary human beings, especially infants and children.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ogre (perempuan: ogress) adalah monster legendaris berwujud makhluk mirip manusia yang besar dan buruk rupa yang memakan manusia, terutama bayi dan anak-anak.",
        "en": "An ogre (female ogress) is a legendary monster, a large, hideous humanoid that eats people, especially babies and children."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c02",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "In mythology, ogres are often depicted as inhumanly large, tall, and having a disproportionately large head, abundant hair, unusually colored skin, a voracious appetite, and a strong body. Ogres are closely linked with giants and with human cannibals in mythology.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ogre sering digambarkan sangat besar dan tinggi, berkepala terlalu besar, berambut lebat, berkulit warna tak biasa, rakus, dan bertubuh kuat, serta erat terkait dengan raksasa dan manusia kanibal.",
        "en": "Ogres are often shown as hugely large and tall, with oversized heads, thick hair, oddly coloured skin, voracious appetites and strong bodies, closely tied to giants and human cannibals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c03",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "Famous examples of ogres in folklore include the ogre in \"Puss in Boots\" and the ogre in \"Hop-o'-My-Thumb\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Contoh terkenal adalah ogre dalam Puss in Boots dan Hop-o'-My-Thumb.",
        "en": "Famous folklore ogres include those in Puss in Boots and Hop-o'-My-Thumb."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c04",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "Other characters sometimes described as ogres include the title character from \"Bluebeard\", the Beast from Beauty and the Beast, Humbaba from the Epic of Gilgamesh, Grendel from Beowulf, Polyphemus the Cyclops from Homer's Odyssey",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tokoh lain yang kadang disebut ogre antara lain Bluebeard, si Buruk Rupa dalam Beauty and the Beast, Humbaba dari Epos Gilgamesh, Grendel dari Beowulf, dan Polifemus dari Odisseia.",
        "en": "Other figures sometimes called ogres include Bluebeard, the Beast of Beauty and the Beast, Humbaba of the Epic of Gilgamesh, Grendel of Beowulf and Polyphemus of the Odyssey."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c05",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "The word ogre is of French origin, originally derived from the Etruscan god Orcus. Its earliest attestation is in Chrétien de Troyes's late 12th-century verse romance Perceval, li contes del graal",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata ogre berasal dari bahasa Prancis, dari nama dewa Etruska Orcus, dan pertama tercatat dalam roman Perceval, li contes del graal karya Chrétien de Troyes akhir abad ke-12.",
        "en": "The word ogre is French, from the Etruscan god Orcus, first attested in Chrétien de Troyes's late 12th-century romance Perceval, li contes del graal."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c06",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "The word orco was widely used in Italy at least since 13th century, as attested by Jacomo Tolomei",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata orco sudah banyak dipakai di Italia setidaknya sejak abad ke-13, seperti dicatat Jacomo Tolomei.",
        "en": "The word orco was widely used in Italy since at least the 13th century, as Jacomo Tolomei attests."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c07",
      "source_id": "ogre-s1",
      "quote": "The Italian author Giambattista Basile (1575–1632) used the related Neapolitan word uerco, or in standard Italian, orco in some of his tales, and first talks of female orcs (e.g. in \"Petrosinella\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pengarang Italia Giambattista Basile (1575–1632) memakai kata Neapolitan uerco atau orco dalam dongengnya dan pertama kali menyebut orco perempuan, misalnya dalam Petrosinella.",
        "en": "The Italian writer Giambattista Basile (1575–1632) used the Neapolitan uerco, or orco, in his tales and first spoke of female ogres, as in Petrosinella."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c08",
      "source_id": "ogre-s2",
      "quote": "Legenda Ogre berawal dari zaman pertengahan di Eropa Selatan, di mana banyak orang barbar yang bersenjatakan pemukul batu yang menyerang wilayah Romawi.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia, legenda ogre berawal dari Abad Pertengahan di Eropa Selatan, saat banyak orang barbar bersenjata pemukul batu menyerang wilayah Romawi.",
        "en": "According to the Indonesian article, the ogre legend began in the Middle Ages in southern Europe, when many club-armed barbarians attacked Roman lands."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c09",
      "source_id": "ogre-s3",
      "quote": "語源としては、古くさかのぼるばあい、ギリシア・ローマ神話の冥府の神オルクスに由来するという説がある。しかし、直接にはイタリア語方言で「悪鬼、怪物」を意味するオルコやオルゴ（イタリア語: Orco, Orgo）に基づくともされる。",

      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, kata ini mungkin berasal dari Orcus, dewa dunia bawah mitologi Yunani-Romawi, tetapi secara langsung dari kata dialek Italia orco atau orgo, \"iblis, monster\".",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, the word may go back to Orcus, the Greco-Roman underworld god, but derives directly from the Italian dialect orco or orgo, 'fiend, monster'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "ogre-c10",
      "source_id": "ogre-s3",
      "quote": "北ヨーロッパでは凶暴で残忍な性格であり、人の生肉を食べるとされる。一方で、引っ込み思案で臆病という面もある。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, di Eropa utara ogre dianggap buas dan kejam serta memakan daging manusia mentah, tetapi juga pemalu dan penakut.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, in northern Europe the ogre is thought fierce and cruel and to eat raw human flesh, yet also shy and timid."

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Hippogriff adalah makhluk setengah elang setengah kuda yang diciptakan Ludovico Ariosto dalam Orlando Furioso sebagai anak griffin dan kuda betina, tunggangan yang dapat terbang ke Bulan.",
    "en": "The hippogriff is a half-eagle, half-horse creature invented by Ludovico Ariosto in Orlando Furioso as the offspring of a griffin and a mare, a steed that can fly to the Moon.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "hippogriff-c01",
      "hippogriff-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Hippogriff atau hippogryph, dari bahasa Yunani híppos, \"kuda\", dan bahasa Italia grifo, \"griffin\", adalah makhluk bertubuh depan elang dan belakang kuda. Ludovico Ariosto menciptakannya pada awal abad ke-16 dalam Orlando Furioso sebagai anak griffin dan kuda betina, gabungan yang dianggap mustahil karena griffin memangsa kuda. Hippogriff milik penyihir Atlantes lalu ditunggangi Ruggiero untuk membebaskan Angelica, dan dapat terbang mengelilingi dunia sampai ke Bulan.",
      "en": "The hippogriff or hippogryph, from Greek híppos, 'horse', and Italian grifo, 'griffin', has an eagle's front and a horse's hindquarters. Ludovico Ariosto invented it early in the 16th century in Orlando Furioso as the offspring of a griffin and a mare, a cross thought impossible since griffins prey on horses. Owned by the sorcerer Atlantes and then ridden by Ruggiero to free Angelica, it can fly round the world and to the Moon.",

      "id": "hippogriff-c01",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "The hippogriff (Italian: ippogrifo) or hippogryph is a legendary creature with the front half of an eagle and the hind half of a horse.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hippogriff (bahasa Italia: ippogrifo) atau hippogryph adalah makhluk legendaris bertubuh depan elang dan bertubuh belakang kuda.",
        "en": "The hippogriff (Italian ippogrifo) or hippogryph is a legendary creature with the front half of an eagle and the hind half of a horse."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c02",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "It was invented at the beginning of the 16th century by Ludovico Ariosto in his Orlando Furioso. Within the poem, the hippogriff is a steed born of a mare and a griffin—something considered impossible. It is extremely fast and is presented as being able to fly around the world and to the Moon.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia diciptakan pada awal abad ke-16 oleh Ludovico Ariosto dalam Orlando Furioso sebagai tunggangan yang lahir dari kuda betina dan griffin, hal yang dianggap mustahil; ia sangat cepat dan dapat terbang mengelilingi dunia sampai ke Bulan.",
        "en": "It was invented early in the 16th century by Ludovico Ariosto in Orlando Furioso as a steed born of a mare and a griffin, thought impossible; it is extremely fast and can fly round the world and to the Moon."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c03",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "Its original owner was the sorcerer Atlantes, a Saracen who lived in Mount Carena in Morocco and the knight errant (his adopted son) Ruggiero, who, from the creature's back, frees the beautiful Angelica.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pemilik pertamanya penyihir Saracen Atlantes dari Gunung Carena di Maroko, lalu ksatria pengembara Ruggiero, anak angkatnya, yang dari punggung hippogriff membebaskan Angelica yang cantik.",
        "en": "Its first owner was the Saracen sorcerer Atlantes of Mount Carena in Morocco, then the knight-errant Ruggiero, his adopted son, who from its back frees the beautiful Angelica."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c04",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "The hippogriff became a subject of visual art in the 19th century, when it was often drawn by Gustave Doré.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Hippogriff menjadi subjek seni rupa pada abad ke-19 dan sering digambar Gustave Doré.",
        "en": "The hippogriff became a subject of art in the 19th century, often drawn by Gustave Doré."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c05",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "The word hippogriff, also spelled hippogryph, is derived from the Ancient Greek: ἵππος híppos, meaning \"horse\", and the Italian grifo meaning \"griffin\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata hippogriff berasal dari bahasa Yunani Kuno híppos, \"kuda\", dan bahasa Italia grifo, \"griffin\".",
        "en": "The word hippogriff comes from Ancient Greek híppos, 'horse', and Italian grifo, 'griffin'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c06",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "The word hippogriff was adopted into English shortly before 1615; prior to this, John Harington's 1591 translation of Orlando furioso called the creature a \"Griffith horse.\"",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata ini masuk ke bahasa Inggris tak lama sebelum 1615; sebelumnya terjemahan John Harington tahun 1591 menyebutnya \"Griffith horse\".",
        "en": "The word entered English shortly before 1615; before that John Harington's 1591 translation called it a 'Griffith horse'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c07",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s1",
      "quote": "Like a griffin, it has the head of an eagle, claws armed with talons, and wings covered with feathers, the rest of its body being that of a horse.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Legends of Charlemagne karya Thomas Bulfinch, seperti griffin ia berkepala elang, bercakar tajam, bersayap berbulu, dan selebihnya bertubuh kuda.",
        "en": "According to Thomas Bulfinch's Legends of Charlemagne, like a griffin it has an eagle's head, taloned claws and feathered wings, the rest of its body being a horse's."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c08",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s2",
      "quote": "beberapa buku dalam serial Harry Potter, seperti dalam kisah Harry Potter and the Prisoner of Azkaban memunculkan Hippogriff seperti misalnya Buckbeak yang dipelihara oleh Hagrid dan menjadi kawan Harry.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia, hippogriff muncul dalam serial Harry Potter, misalnya Buckbeak peliharaan Hagrid dalam Harry Potter and the Prisoner of Azkaban.",
        "en": "According to the Indonesian article, hippogriffs appear in the Harry Potter series, such as Hagrid's Buckbeak in Harry Potter and the Prisoner of Azkaban."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "hippogriff-c09",
      "source_id": "hippogriff-s3",
      "quote": "グリフォンが馬を好んで食べるということから、ありえないもの（天敵と被食者のハーフ）の代名詞だったものが、そのまま想像上の生物の名前になってしまったという説もある。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, karena griffin suka memakan kuda, gabungan keduanya dulu menjadi ungkapan untuk hal mustahil, dan menurut satu teori lalu menjadi nama makhluk khayalan.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, since griffins like to eat horses, a cross of the two once stood for the impossible, and by one theory became the name of an imaginary creature."
      }
    }

      "id": "Wyvern atau wivern adalah naga berkaki dua, bersayap dua, dan sering berekor runcing, penting dalam heraldik dan kini menjadi maskot sekolah dan tim olahraga. Berbeda dari naga lain, ia jarang menyemburkan api, meski menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia bergantung budayanya ia bisa menyemburkan api atau berbisa; ada pula wivern laut berekor sirip ikan.       "en": "The wyvern or wivern is a dragon with two legs, two wings and often a pointed tail, important in heraldry and now a mascot of schools and sports teams. Unlike other dragons it rarely breathes fire, though the Indonesian article says by culture it may breathe fire or carry venom; there is also a fish-tailed sea-wyvern.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c01",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "The wyvern ... sometimes spelled wivern ... is a type of mythical  dragon with two legs, two wings, and often a pointed tail.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wyvern atau wivern adalah sejenis naga mitis berkaki dua, bersayap dua, dan sering berekor runcing.",
        "en": "The wyvern or wivern is a kind of mythical dragon with two legs, two wings and often a pointed tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c02",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "The wyvern in its various forms is important in heraldry, frequently appearing as a mascot of schools and athletic teams (chiefly in the United States, United Kingdom, and Canada).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Wyvern penting dalam heraldik dan sering menjadi maskot sekolah dan tim olahraga, terutama di Amerika Serikat, Britania Raya, dan Kanada.",
        "en": "The wyvern is important in heraldry and often a mascot of schools and sports teams, mainly in the United States, United Kingdom and Canada."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c03",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike other dragons.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berbeda dari naga lain, wyvern dalam heraldik dan cerita rakyat jarang menyemburkan api.",
        "en": "Unlike other dragons, the wyvern of heraldry and folklore rarely breathes fire."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c04",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "According to the Oxford English Dictionary, the word is a development of Middle English wyver (attested fourteenth century), from Anglo-French wivre (compare French: vouivre and guivre), which originate from Latin: vīpera, meaning \"viper\", \"adder\", or \"asp\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Oxford English Dictionary, kata ini berkembang dari bahasa Inggris Pertengahan wyver (abad ke-14), dari bahasa Anglo-Prancis wivre (bandingkan vouivre dan guivre), yang berasal dari bahasa Latin vīpera, \"ular berbisa\".",
        "en": "According to the Oxford English Dictionary, the word developed from Middle English wyver (14th century), from Anglo-French wivre (compare vouivre and guivre), from Latin vīpera, 'viper'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c05",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "The concluding \"–n\" had been added by the beginning of the 17th century, when John Guillim in 1610 describes the \"wiverne\" as a creature that \"partake[s] of a Fowle in the Wings and Legs ... and doth resemble a Serpent in the Taile\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Akhiran -n sudah ditambahkan pada awal abad ke-17, ketika John Guillim pada 1610 menggambarkan \"wiverne\" sebagai makhluk bersayap dan berkaki burung dengan ekor seperti ular.",
        "en": "The final -n was added by the early 17th century, when John Guillim in 1610 described the 'wiverne' as having a fowl's wings and legs and a serpent's tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c06",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "By noting physical resemblances between the javelins and snakes, he proposed that the images of \"venomous snake\" and \"light javelin\" were melded to produce the novel concept of a flying snake.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Medievalis William Sayers mengusulkan bahwa gambaran \"ular berbisa\" dan \"lembing ringan\" (guivre) berpadu menjadi konsep baru ular terbang.",
        "en": "The medievalist William Sayers proposed that the images of 'venomous snake' and 'light javelin' (guivre) merged into the new idea of a flying snake."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c07",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s1",
      "quote": "The Egyptian goddess Wadjet, depicted as a winged cobra or uraeus, exemplifies this tradition and served as a protective deity of Lower Egypt from the Predynastic period onwards.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konsep ular bersayap ada di banyak budaya kuno Mediterania dan Timur Dekat, misalnya dewi Mesir Wadjet, kobra bersayap pelindung Mesir Hilir sejak masa Pradinasti.",
        "en": "Winged snakes appear across ancient Mediterranean and Near Eastern cultures, for example the Egyptian goddess Wadjet, a winged cobra protecting Lower Egypt from Predynastic times."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c08",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s2",
      "quote": "Tergantung budaya bersangkutan, wivern dapat dideskripsikan menyemburkan api atau memiliki bisa mematikan, atau memiliki dua kemampuan tersebut atau tidak sama sekali.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia, bergantung budayanya wivern dapat menyemburkan api, memiliki bisa mematikan, keduanya, atau tidak sama sekali.",
        "en": "According to the Indonesian article, depending on the culture the wivern may breathe fire, have deadly venom, both, or neither."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c09",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s2",
      "quote": "Ada varian wivern yang menghuni laut, disebut Wivern laut yang memiliki sirip ikan sebagai pengganti ekor naganya yang melingkar.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada varian penghuni laut, wivern laut, yang bersirip ikan sebagai ganti ekor naga yang melingkar.",
        "en": "There is a sea variant, the sea-wyvern, with a fish's fin in place of its coiled dragon tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c10",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s3",
      "quote": "「二足の竜」の図像は、イギリスを除くヨーロッパではドラゴンの一般的な形態の一つとして扱われており、これをワイバーンとしてドラゴンから区別するのはイギリスおよびイギリスの旧植民地に特有のことである。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, di Eropa selain Inggris naga berkaki dua dianggap bentuk naga biasa; membedakannya sebagai wyvern adalah kekhasan Inggris dan bekas jajahannya.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, outside Britain two-legged dragons are treated as ordinary dragons in Europe; distinguishing them as wyverns is peculiar to Britain and its former colonies."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "wyvern-c11",
      "source_id": "wyvern-s3",
      "quote": "異説として、ワイバーンをラテン語の viverra（フェレット）と同一視する者があった。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada pula yang dahulu menyamakan wyvern dengan kata Latin viverra, \"musang\", sebuah etimologi rakyat.",
        "en": "Some once equated wyvern with Latin viverra, 'ferret', a folk etymology."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "The tooth fairy is a Western childhood figure who comes while a child sleeps and swaps a milk tooth under the pillow for money, first recorded in 1908.",
    "claim_ids": [

    {
      "id": "Peri gigi adalah tokoh masa kanak-kanak dalam budaya Barat: anak menaruh gigi susu yang tanggal di bawah bantal atau di meja samping ranjang, lalu peri gigi menggantinya dengan sedikit uang saat ia tidur. Catatan tertua tradisi ini berasal dari Chicago Daily Tribune tahun 1908. Takhayul lebih tua tentang gigi anak antara lain pembakaran gigi susu di Inggris dan ketakutan bahwa penyihir dapat menguasai orang lewat giginya.",
      "en": "The tooth fairy is a childhood figure of Western culture: a child puts a lost milk tooth under the pillow or on the bedside table, and the tooth fairy swaps it for a little money while the child sleeps. Older superstitions about children's teeth include burning milk teeth in England and fear that witches could control people through their teeth.",

      "en": "Its looks are not fixed; Rosemary Wells's 1984 study found most people imagine it female. Counterparts elsewhere include la Petite Souris and Ratoncito Pérez, while in Japan milk teeth are thrown onto the roof or under the floor.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c01",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "The tooth fairy is a folkloric figure of early childhood in Western and Western-influenced cultures. The folklore states that when children lose one of their baby teeth, they should place it underneath their pillow or on their bedside table; the tooth fairy will visit while they sleep, replacing the lost tooth with a small payment.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Peri gigi adalah tokoh cerita rakyat masa kanak-kanak dalam budaya Barat dan yang terpengaruh Barat: anak yang tanggal gigi susunya menaruhnya di bawah bantal atau di meja samping ranjang, lalu peri gigi datang saat ia tidur dan menggantinya dengan sedikit uang.",
        "en": "The tooth fairy is a childhood folklore figure in Western and Western-influenced cultures: a child who loses a milk tooth puts it under the pillow or on the bedside table, and the tooth fairy comes during sleep and replaces it with a small payment."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c02",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "Children in England were instructed to burn their baby teeth, on pain of spending eternity searching for the baby teeth in the afterlife. Fear of witches was another reason to bury or burn teeth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Pada Abad Pertengahan anak-anak di Inggris disuruh membakar gigi susunya agar tidak selamanya mencarinya di alam baka, dan ketakutan pada penyihir juga menjadi alasan gigi dikubur atau dibakar.",
        "en": "In the Middle Ages English children were told to burn their milk teeth or spend eternity searching for them in the afterlife, and fear of witches was another reason to bury or burn teeth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c03",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "In medieval Europe, it was thought that a witch could assume total power over someone if they were to obtain one of their teeth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Eropa abad pertengahan, penyihir dipercaya dapat menguasai seseorang sepenuhnya bila memperoleh salah satu giginya.",
        "en": "In medieval Europe a witch was thought to gain total power over someone by obtaining one of their teeth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c04",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "While these beliefs may have had an influence, the earliest known record of the tooth fairy tradition itself is a 1908 \"Household Hints\" item in the Chicago Daily Tribune",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Catatan tertua tradisi peri gigi sendiri adalah rubrik \"Household Hints\" tahun 1908 di Chicago Daily Tribune.",
        "en": "The earliest known record of the tooth fairy tradition itself is a 1908 'Household Hints' item in the Chicago Daily Tribune."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c05",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "Unlike Santa Claus and, to a lesser extent, the Easter Bunny, there are few details of the tooth fairy's appearance that are consistent in various versions of the myth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Berbeda dari Sinterklas dan Kelinci Paskah, hanya sedikit rincian rupa peri gigi yang sama di berbagai versi.",
        "en": "Unlike Santa Claus and the Easter Bunny, few details of the tooth fairy's looks are consistent across versions."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c06",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s1",
      "quote": "A 1984 study conducted by Rosemary Wells revealed that most – 74% of those surveyed – believed the tooth fairy to be female, while 12% believed the tooth fairy to be neither male nor female, and 8% believed the tooth fairy could be either male or female.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kajian Rosemary Wells tahun 1984 menemukan 74% responden menganggap peri gigi perempuan, 12% bukan lelaki maupun perempuan, dan 8% bisa keduanya.",
        "en": "A 1984 study by Rosemary Wells found 74% of respondents thought the tooth fairy female, 12% neither male nor female, and 8% either."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c07",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s2",
      "quote": "Tradisi meninggalkan gigi di bawah bantal untuk peri gigi dipraktikkan di berbagai negara di Anglosfer.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Indonesia, tradisi menaruh gigi di bawah bantal untuk peri gigi dipraktikkan di berbagai negara Anglosfer.",
        "en": "According to the Indonesian article, leaving a tooth under the pillow for the tooth fairy is practised across the Anglosphere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c08",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s3",
      "quote": "歯の妖精（ドイツ語: Zahnfee, 英語: Tooth fairy, フランス語: la Petite Souris, スペイン語: Ratoncito Pérez）とは、抜けた乳歯をコイン（場合によってはプレゼント）に交換してくれる妖精である。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, peri gigi (Jerman: Zahnfee; Prancis: la Petite Souris; Spanyol: Ratoncito Pérez) menukar gigi susu yang tanggal dengan koin atau hadiah.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, the tooth fairy (German Zahnfee, French la Petite Souris, Spanish Ratoncito Pérez) exchanges lost milk teeth for coins or gifts."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "tooth-fairy-c09",
      "source_id": "tooth-fairy-s3",
      "quote": "日本では、抜けた乳歯を、上の歯の場合には床下に、下の歯の場合には屋根に向かって縁側や窓などから放り投げることが行われる。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Di Jepang, gigi susu atas yang tanggal dilempar ke kolong lantai dan gigi bawah ke atap dari beranda atau jendela.",
        "en": "In Japan a lost upper milk tooth is thrown under the floor and a lower one towards the roof from a veranda or window."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "Elementals are supernatural beings of Renaissance occult and alchemical tradition, above all Paracelsus, who divided them into gnomes, undines, sylphs and salamanders for the four elements.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "elemental-c01",
      "elemental-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Elemental adalah makhluk gaib yang dikembangkan dalam karya okultisme dan alkimia Renaisans, terutama oleh Paracelsus pada abad ke-16. Ia membaginya menjadi gnome (tanah), undine (air), sylph (udara), dan salamander (api) dalam bukunya yang terbit pada 1566, dan menganggap mereka makhluk di antara roh dan manusia: tak terlihat tetapi bertubuh, makan, tidur, dan berpakaian.",
      "en": "Elementals are supernatural beings developed in Renaissance occult and alchemical works, above all by Paracelsus in the 16th century. He divided them into gnomes (earth), undines (water), sylphs (air) and salamanders (fire) in his book printed in 1566, and saw them as between spirits and humans: invisible yet bodily, eating, sleeping and wearing clothes.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "elemental-c01",
        "elemental-c02",
        "elemental-c03",
        "elemental-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Artikel bahasa Jerman menelusuri gagasan ini ke Neoplatonisme akhir zaman kuno dan menegaskan bahwa elemental adalah tokoh filsafat dan sastra, bukan cerita rakyat; Paracelsus sendiri tidak memakai istilah elemental. Le Comte de Gabalis (1670) membahas pernikahan dengan mereka, dan kini mereka muncul dalam esoterisme modern seperti antroposofi.",
      "en": "The German article traces the idea to late-antique Neoplatonism and stresses that elementals are figures of philosophy and literature, not folklore; Paracelsus himself did not use the word elemental. Le Comte de Gabalis (1670) discusses marrying them, and today they appear in modern esotericism such as anthroposophy.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "elemental-c01",
      "source_id": "elemental-s1",
      "quote": "An elemental is a mythic supernatural being that is described in occult and alchemical works from around the time of the European Renaissance, and particularly elaborated in the 16th century works of Paracelsus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Elemental adalah makhluk gaib mitis yang digambarkan dalam karya okultisme dan alkimia sekitar masa Renaisans Eropa, terutama dikembangkan dalam karya Paracelsus abad ke-16.",
        "en": "An elemental is a mythic supernatural being described in occult and alchemical works from around the European Renaissance, especially developed in Paracelsus's 16th-century writings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c02",
      "source_id": "elemental-s1",
      "quote": "According to Paracelsus and his subsequent followers, there are four categories of elementals, which are gnomes, undines, sylphs, and salamanders. These correspond to the four Empedoclean elements of antiquity: earth, water, air, and fire, respectively.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Paracelsus dan pengikutnya, ada empat jenis elemental, yaitu gnome, undine, sylph, dan salamander, yang sesuai dengan empat unsur Empedokles: tanah, air, udara, dan api.",
        "en": "According to Paracelsus and his followers there are four kinds of elementals, gnomes, undines, sylphs and salamanders, matching the four Empedoclean elements: earth, water, air and fire."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c03",
      "source_id": "elemental-s1",
      "quote": "In his 16th-century work A Book on Nymphs, Sylphs, Pygmies, and Salamanders, and on the Other Spirits, Paracelsus identified mythological beings as belonging to one of the four elements. Part of the Philosophia Magna, this book was first printed in 1566 after Paracelsus' death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam A Book on Nymphs, Sylphs, Pygmies, and Salamanders, and on the Other Spirits, Paracelsus menggolongkan makhluk mitologis ke dalam empat unsur; buku ini bagian Philosophia Magna dan pertama dicetak pada 1566 setelah ia wafat.",
        "en": "In his Book on Nymphs, Sylphs, Pygmies, and Salamanders, and on the Other Spirits, Paracelsus assigned mythological beings to the four elements; part of the Philosophia Magna, it was first printed in 1566 after his death."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c04",
      "source_id": "elemental-s1",
      "quote": "The concept of elementals seems to have been conceived by Paracelsus in the 16th century, though he did not in fact use the term \"elemental\" or a German equivalent.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konsep elemental tampaknya diciptakan Paracelsus pada abad ke-16, walau ia sendiri tidak memakai istilah elemental atau padanannya dalam bahasa Jerman.",
        "en": "The concept of elementals seems to have been devised by Paracelsus in the 16th century, though he did not actually use the word 'elemental' or a German equivalent."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c05",
      "source_id": "elemental-s1",
      "quote": "He regarded them not so much as spirits but as beings between creatures and spirits, generally being invisible to mankind but having physical and commonly humanoid bodies, as well as eating, sleeping, and wearing clothes like humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia menganggap mereka bukan roh semata, melainkan makhluk di antara makhluk jasmani dan roh, biasanya tak terlihat manusia tetapi bertubuh jasmani mirip manusia, makan, tidur, dan berpakaian seperti manusia.",
        "en": "He saw them not so much as spirits but as beings between creatures and spirits, usually invisible to humans yet with physical, mostly humanlike bodies, eating, sleeping and wearing clothes like people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c06",
      "source_id": "elemental-s2",
      "quote": "Die Vorstellung von Elementargeistern stammt ursprünglich aus dem spätantiken Neuplatonismus und wurde von frühneuzeitlichen Naturphilosophen wieder aufgegriffen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, gagasan roh unsur berasal dari Neoplatonisme akhir zaman kuno dan dihidupkan kembali para filsuf alam awal zaman modern.",
        "en": "According to the German article, the idea of elemental spirits comes from late-antique Neoplatonism and was revived by early modern natural philosophers."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c07",
      "source_id": "elemental-s2",
      "quote": "Elementargeister sind Gestalten der Naturphilosophie und der Literatur, in Volksglauben und Volkserzählungen kommen sie nicht vor.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh unsur adalah tokoh filsafat alam dan sastra, bukan bagian kepercayaan atau cerita rakyat.",
        "en": "Elemental spirits are figures of natural philosophy and literature and do not occur in folk belief or folk tales."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c08",
      "source_id": "elemental-s2",
      "quote": "Elementargeister spielen auch in verschiedenen Formen von Neuheidentum und moderner Esoterik eine Rolle, etwa in der Anthroposophie.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka juga berperan dalam berbagai bentuk neopaganisme dan esoterisme modern, misalnya antroposofi.",
        "en": "They also play a role in various forms of neopaganism and modern esotericism, such as anthroposophy."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "elemental-c09",
      "source_id": "elemental-s3",
      "quote": "その1世紀後にパリで出版されたモンフォーコン・ド・ヴィラール ... の隠秘学小説『ガバリス伯爵』（1670年）は、作中人物のガバリス伯爵をして「四大の民」との婚姻について語らしめた。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, novel okultisme Montfaucon de Villars, Le Comte de Gabalis (Paris, 1670), memuat tokoh Comte de Gabalis yang membahas pernikahan dengan \"bangsa empat unsur\".",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, Montfaucon de Villars's occult novel Le Comte de Gabalis (Paris, 1670) has its title character discuss marriage with the 'peoples of the four elements'."
      }
    }
  ],

    "en": "The cockatrice is a two-legged rooster-headed dragon or serpent of English myth that hatches from an egg brooded by a toad and kills with a glance.",

      "id": "Cockatrice adalah naga berkaki dua atau makhluk mirip ular berkepala ayam jantan, menurut artikel bahasa Prancis bersayap kelelawar. Ia membunuh dengan sentuhan, napas, atau tatapan, tetapi musang kebal terhadapnya dan ia mati mendengar kokok ayam jantan.",
      "en": "The cockatrice is a two-legged dragon or serpent-like creature with a rooster's head, bat-winged in the French article.   "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c01",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "A cockatrice is a mythical beast, essentially a two-legged dragon, wyvern, or serpent-like creature with a rooster's head.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cockatrice adalah binatang mitis, pada dasarnya naga berkaki dua, wyvern, atau makhluk mirip ular berkepala ayam jantan.",
        "en": "A cockatrice is a mythical beast, essentially a two-legged dragon, wyvern or serpent-like creature with a rooster's head."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c02",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "Described by Laurence Breiner as \"an ornament in the drama and poetry of the Elizabethans\", it was featured prominently in English thought and myth for centuries. According to legend, they are created by a chicken egg hatched by a toad or snake.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Laurence Breiner menyebutnya \"hiasan dalam drama dan puisi era Elizabeth\"; ia menonjol dalam pemikiran dan mitos Inggris selama berabad-abad, dan menurut legenda lahir dari telur ayam yang dierami kodok atau ular.",
        "en": "Laurence Breiner called it 'an ornament in the drama and poetry of the Elizabethans'; it was prominent in English thought and myth for centuries, and by legend hatches from a chicken egg incubated by a toad or snake."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c03",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "The first English mention of the cockatrice was in the 14th-century John Wycliffe translation of the Bible. The word was used for the translation of various Hebrew words for asp and adder in the Book of Isaiah 11, 14 and 59.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebutan Inggris pertama ada dalam terjemahan Alkitab John Wycliffe abad ke-14, untuk menerjemahkan berbagai kata Ibrani bagi ular berbisa dalam Kitab Yesaya 11, 14, dan 59.",
        "en": "The first English mention is in John Wycliffe's 14th-century Bible translation, used for various Hebrew words for asp and adder in Isaiah 11, 14 and 59."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c04",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "The Oxford English Dictionary gives a derivation from Old French cocatris, from medieval Latin calcatrix, a translation of the Greek ichneumon, meaning tracker.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut Oxford English Dictionary, kata ini berasal dari bahasa Prancis Kuno cocatris, dari bahasa Latin abad pertengahan calcatrix, terjemahan kata Yunani ichneumon, \"pelacak\".",
        "en": "The Oxford English Dictionary derives it from Old French cocatris, from medieval Latin calcatrix, a translation of Greek ichneumon, 'tracker'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c05",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "Cockatrice became seen as synonymous with basilisk when the basiliscus in Bartholomeus Anglicus's De proprietatibus rerum (ca 1260) was translated by John Trevisa as cockatrice (1397).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cockatrice mulai disamakan dengan basilisk ketika basiliscus dalam De proprietatibus rerum karya Bartholomeus Anglicus (sekitar 1260) diterjemahkan John Trevisa sebagai cockatrice (1397).",
        "en": "The cockatrice came to be equated with the basilisk when the basiliscus in Bartholomeus Anglicus's De proprietatibus rerum (c. 1260) was translated by John Trevisa as cockatrice (1397)."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c06",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "According to myth, the cockatrice can kill people by touch, breath, or a glance, a trait referred to in literature as \"the death-darting eye of Cockatrice\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cockatrice dapat membunuh dengan sentuhan, napas, atau tatapan, yang dalam sastra disebut \"mata cockatrice yang melemparkan maut\".",
        "en": "The cockatrice can kill by touch, breath or a glance, called in literature 'the death-darting eye of Cockatrice'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c07",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "It was repeated in the late-medieval bestiaries that the weasel is the only animal that is immune to the glance of a cockatrice. It was also thought that a cockatrice would die instantly upon hearing a rooster crow",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bestiari akhir abad pertengahan menyebut musang satu-satunya hewan yang kebal terhadap tatapannya, dan cockatrice dipercaya langsung mati mendengar kokok ayam jantan.",
        "en": "Late-medieval bestiaries repeat that the weasel alone is immune to its glance, and it was thought to die instantly on hearing a rooster crow."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c08",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s1",
      "quote": "It is thought that a cock egg would hatch a cockatrice, and this could be prevented by tossing the egg over the family house, landing on the other side of the house, without allowing the egg to hit the house.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Telur ayam jantan dipercaya menetaskan cockatrice, dan hal itu dapat dicegah dengan melemparkan telur melewati atap rumah tanpa mengenai rumah.",
        "en": "A cock's egg was thought to hatch a cockatrice, which could be prevented by throwing the egg over the house without letting it hit the house."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c09",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s2",
      "quote": "Le cocatrix, coquatrix, caucatrix ou cocatrice est un animal fabuleux qui possède selon la légende une tête de coq, des ailes de chauve-souris et un corps de serpent ou de coq.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, cocatrix (juga coquatrix atau caucatrix) berkepala ayam jantan, bersayap kelelawar, dan bertubuh ular atau ayam.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the cocatrix (also coquatrix or caucatrix) has a rooster's head, bat's wings and a serpent's or cock's body."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c10",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s2",
      "quote": "Selon certaines sources, la cocatrix serait « née par accident à la fin du XIIe siècle et est morte au milieu du  XVIIe siècle, victime de la science ».",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebagian sumber menyebut cocatrix \"lahir secara kebetulan pada akhir abad ke-12 dan mati pada pertengahan abad ke-17, korban ilmu pengetahuan\".",
        "en": "Some sources say the cocatrix was 'born by accident at the end of the 12th century and died in the mid-17th, a victim of science'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c11",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s3",
      "quote": "雄鶏の産んだ卵をヒキガエルが温めて生まれるという（雄鶏は7歳で、卵はヒキガエルが9年間温めるとされる）。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, ia lahir dari telur ayam jantan yang dierami kodok; ayam jantan itu berumur 7 tahun dan kodok mengeraminya 9 tahun.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, it hatches from a rooster's egg incubated by a toad; the rooster is 7 years old and the toad broods it for 9 years."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cockatrice-c12",
      "source_id": "cockatrice-s3",
      "quote": "ウィリアム・シェイクスピアの『十二夜』の中でも、登場人物がコカトリスについて言及している。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dalam Twelfth Night karya William Shakespeare, seorang tokoh menyebut cockatrice.",
        "en": "In William Shakespeare's Twelfth Night a character mentions the cockatrice."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Naga Eropa adalah makhluk legendaris bersisik, bersayap kelelawar, dan penyembur api dari cerita rakyat Eropa, penjaga harta yang biasanya dibunuh seorang pahlawan.",

    {
      "id": "Naga Eropa adalah makhluk legendaris dalam cerita rakyat dan mitologi Eropa. Sejak Abad Pertengahan Awal ia biasanya digambarkan besar, mirip kadal, bersisik, bertanduk, bersayap kulit seperti kelelawar, berekor panjang, dan menyemburkan api. Ia menjaga gua atau kastel penuh harta; naga jahat dilawan pahlawan, seperti Fáfnir yang dibunuh Sigurð dari lubang, sedangkan naga baik memberi nasihat.",
      "en": "The European dragon is a legendary creature of European folklore and myth. Since the Early Middle Ages it has usually been shown as large, lizard-like, scaly, horned, with leathery bat wings, a long tail and fiery breath. It guards caves or castles full of treasure; evil dragons are fought by heroes, as Fáfnir was slain by Sigurð from a pit, while good ones give counsel.",

Naga Romawi berkembang dari ular Yunani dan naga Timur Dekat; Python dan Hydra sebenarnya digambarkan sebagai ular. Naga berkaki dua disebut wyvern dan naga tak bersayap worm.",
      "en": "The word dragon comes from Greek drákōn, 'the staring one'; in Virgil's day draco and serpens could mean the same, and the Germanic worm or wyrm also means snake.
