  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Dahu adalah binatang khayalan mirip kambing gunung dari Prancis dan Swiss yang kakinya di satu sisi lebih pendek, sasaran perburuan pura-pura untuk mengerjai orang lugu.",
    "en": "The dahu is an imaginary mountain-goat-like animal of France and Switzerland with legs shorter on one side, the target of mock hunts to fool the gullible.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "dahu-c01",
      "dahu-c03",
      "dahu-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Dahu adalah makhluk legendaris mirip kambing gunung yang terkenal di Prancis dan daerah berbahasa Prancis di Swiss dan Italia, mungkin terilhami chamois. Kakinya di satu sisi lebih pendek agar dapat berjalan di lereng, sehingga ada dahu yang hanya bisa mengitari gunung berlawanan arah jarum jam dan ada yang searah. Ia kadang digambarkan sebagai campuran musang, kelinci, tupai, burung, dan kambing.",
      "en": "The dahu is a legendary mountain-goat-like creature well known in France and the French-speaking parts of Switzerland and Italy, perhaps inspired by the chamois. Its legs are shorter on one side for walking on slopes, so some dahus can only circle a mountain anticlockwise and others clockwise. It is sometimes described as a mix of weasel, rabbit, squirrel, bird and goat.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "dahu-c01",
        "dahu-c02",
        "dahu-c03",
        "dahu-c04",
        "dahu-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Perburuan dahu adalah lelucon terhadap orang lugu atau orang kota: korban diajak malam-malam ke gunung dan ditinggalkan, atau disuruh memegang karung sementara orang lain menirukan suara dahu agar binatang itu menoleh dan berguling jatuh. Di Jura ia disebut dahut atau dairi, dan ia padanan Wolpertinger Bayern.",
      "en": "The dahu hunt is a prank on the naive or on city people: the victim is taken up the mountain at night and abandoned, or told to hold a sack while another imitates the dahu's call so it turns and tumbles down. In the Jura it is the dahut or dairi, and it is the counterpart of the Bavarian Wolpertinger.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "dahu-c01",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "The dahu (French pronunciation: [da.y]) is a legendary creature that resembles a mountain goat and is well known in France and francophone regions of Switzerland and Italy, including the Aosta Valley.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dahu adalah makhluk legendaris mirip kambing gunung yang terkenal di Prancis serta daerah berbahasa Prancis di Swiss dan Italia, termasuk Lembah Aosta.",
        "en": "The dahu is a legendary creature like a mountain goat, well known in France and the French-speaking parts of Switzerland and Italy, including the Aosta Valley."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c02",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "The dahu, a quadrupedal mammal, may have been inspired by the chamois, a small, horned goat-antelope once plentiful in European mountainous regions, and also resembles the ibex.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dahu mungkin terilhami chamois, kambing-antelop bertanduk kecil yang dulu banyak di pegunungan Eropa, dan juga mirip ibex.",
        "en": "The dahu may have been inspired by the chamois, a small horned goat-antelope once common in European mountains, and also resembles the ibex."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c03",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "The dahu's principal distinguishing characteristic is that the legs on one side of its body are shorter than the legs on the opposite side, to facilitate standing on and walking on steep mountain slopes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ciri utamanya adalah kaki di satu sisi tubuh lebih pendek daripada sisi lain agar mudah berdiri dan berjalan di lereng gunung yang curam.",
        "en": "Its main feature is that the legs on one side of its body are shorter than on the other, to help it stand and walk on steep slopes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c04",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "Therefore, there are two different types of dahu: the laevogyrous dahu, with shorter legs on its left side, walks around the mountain counterclockwise; the dextrogyre dahu, with the shorter legs on its right side, walks clockwise around the mountain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena itu ada dua jenis dahu: dahu levogir berkaki kiri pendek yang berjalan mengitari gunung berlawanan arah jarum jam, dan dahu dekstrogir berkaki kanan pendek yang berjalan searah jarum jam.",
        "en": "So there are two kinds: the laevogyrous dahu, with shorter left legs, circles the mountain anticlockwise, and the dextrogyre dahu, with shorter right legs, clockwise."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c05",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "The \"dahu hunt\" (French: chasse au dahu), similar to another wildlife-related practical joke, the snipe hunt, is a prank in which pranksters may take a victim out at night with the stated intention of catching a dahu only to abandon the victim on the mountain.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "\"Perburuan dahu\" (chasse au dahu), mirip lelucon snipe hunt, adalah keisengan di mana korban diajak keluar malam-malam untuk menangkap dahu lalu ditinggalkan di gunung.",
        "en": "The 'dahu hunt' (chasse au dahu), like the snipe hunt, is a prank in which the victim is taken out at night to catch a dahu and then abandoned on the mountain."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c06",
      "source_id": "dahu-s1",
      "quote": "When the dahu turns around to see the source of the sound, it will lose its balance and roll down the slope to the person with the bag.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Konon dahu yang menoleh ke arah suara tiruan akan kehilangan keseimbangan dan berguling ke bawah lereng ke arah orang yang memegang karung.",
        "en": "Supposedly, when the dahu turns towards an imitated call, it loses its balance and rolls down the slope to the person with the sack."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c07",
      "source_id": "dahu-s2",
      "quote": "Der Dahu (schweiz. auch das Dahu, auch Dahut, Däjü oder Dairi im Jura), Darou in den Vogesen oder Tamarou in den Pyrenäen – ist ein Fabelwesen und eine frankophone Entsprechung zum bayerischen Wolpertinger beziehungsweise dem Hanghuhn.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, dahu (juga Dahut, Däjü, atau Dairi di Jura, Darou di Vosges, Tamarou di Pyrenees) adalah makhluk dongeng padanan berbahasa Prancis bagi Wolpertinger Bayern.",
        "en": "According to the German article, the dahu (also Dahut, Däjü or Dairi in the Jura, Darou in the Vosges, Tamarou in the Pyrenees) is a fabulous creature, the French-speaking counterpart of the Bavarian Wolpertinger."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c08",
      "source_id": "dahu-s2",
      "quote": "Der Dahu wird manchmal als gämsenähnliches Tier beschrieben, manchmal als Mischform von Wieseln, Kaninchen, Eichhörnchen, Vögeln und Ziegen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dahu kadang digambarkan mirip chamois, kadang sebagai campuran musang, kelinci, tupai, burung, dan kambing.",
        "en": "The dahu is sometimes described as chamois-like, sometimes as a mix of weasel, rabbit, squirrel, bird and goat."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "dahu-c09",
      "source_id": "dahu-s3",
      "quote": "L'existence du dahu est généralement évoquée en milieu rural et par plaisanterie auprès de personnes particulièrement naïves et de citadins peu au fait, par exemple, de la faune montagnarde",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, keberadaan dahu biasanya disebut di pedesaan sebagai lelucon kepada orang yang lugu atau orang kota yang tidak mengenal satwa pegunungan.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the dahu is usually brought up in the countryside as a joke on naive people or city folk unfamiliar with mountain wildlife."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Alerion adalah burung heraldik elang tanpa paruh dan cakar, dan dalam bestiari abad pertengahan burung mitis yang hanya sepasang hidup pada satu waktu dan menenggelamkan diri setelah telurnya menetas.",
    "en": "The alerion is a heraldic eagle without beak or claws, and in medieval bestiaries a mythical bird of which only one pair lives at a time, drowning itself once its eggs hatch.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "alerion-c01",
      "alerion-c02",
      "alerion-c03"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Alerion atau avalerion awalnya istilah heraldik untuk elang, lalu untuk anak elang yang digambarkan tanpa paruh dan cakar; tiga alerion menghiasi lambang Lorraine sejak abad ke-13, konon terilhami kisah Godefroy dari Bouillon yang memanah tiga anak elang putih. Asal katanya mungkin dari bahasa Jerman Adler, \"elang\".",
      "en": "Alerion or avalerion was first a heraldic term for an eagle, then for an eaglet shown without beak or claws; three alerions have adorned the arms of Lorraine since the 13th century, supposedly from a tale of Godfrey of Bouillon shooting three white eaglets. The word may come from German Adler, 'eagle'.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "alerion-c01",
        "alerion-c02",
        "alerion-c05",
        "alerion-c06",
        "alerion-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Dalam bestiari abad pertengahan alerion adalah burung mitis sedikit lebih besar dari elang yang hanya sepasang hidup pada satu waktu; mereka bertelur dua butir setiap 60 tahun dan menenggelamkan diri setelah telur menetas. Peta Hereford menempatkannya dekat Hydaspes dan Indus.",
      "en": "In medieval bestiaries the alerion is a mythical bird somewhat larger than an eagle, of which only one pair lives at a time; they lay two eggs every 60 years and drown themselves once these hatch. The Hereford Map places it near the Hydaspes and the Indus.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "alerion-c03",
        "alerion-c04",
        "alerion-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,
  "etymology": {
    "original_form": "Adler / Adelar",

    {
      "id": "alerion-s1",

    {
      "id": "alerion-s2",
      "url": "https://id.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alerion",
      "title": "Alerion",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (id)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "id",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-s3",
      "url": "https://de.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al%C3%A9rion",
      "title": "Alérion",
      "author": null,
      "publisher": "Wikipedia (de)",
      "published": null,
      "language": "de",
      "type": "wikipedia",
      "accessed": "2026-10-02"
    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "alerion-c01",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "Alerion (sometimes known as Avalerion) is a term for a heraldic bird. Historically, it referred to the regular heraldic eagle. Later, heralds used the term alerion to refer to \"baby eagles\" or \"eaglets\".",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Alerion (kadang Avalerion) adalah istilah untuk burung heraldik; awalnya merujuk elang heraldik biasa, lalu dipakai para ahli heraldik untuk \"anak elang\".",
        "en": "Alerion (sometimes Avalerion) is a term for a heraldic bird; it first meant the ordinary heraldic eagle and later 'eaglets'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c02",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "To differentiate them from mature eagles, alerions were shown as an eagle displayed inverted without a beak or claws (disarmed).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk membedakannya dari elang dewasa, alerion digambarkan sebagai elang tanpa paruh dan cakar.",
        "en": "To distinguish them from mature eagles, alerions were shown as eagles without beak or claws."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c03",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "Medieval bestiaries use alerion for a mythological bird described as somewhat larger than an eagle of which only a single pair was said to live at any time. A pair of eggs was laid every 60 years; after hatching, the parents drowned themselves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Bestiari abad pertengahan memakai nama alerion untuk burung mitologis yang sedikit lebih besar dari elang, yang hanya sepasang hidup pada satu waktu; mereka bertelur dua butir setiap 60 tahun, dan setelah telur menetas induknya menenggelamkan diri.",
        "en": "Medieval bestiaries use alerion for a mythological bird somewhat larger than an eagle, of which only one pair lived at a time; they laid two eggs every 60 years, and after hatching the parents drowned themselves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c04",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "The term avalerion is used on the Hereford Map near the Hydaspes and the Indus, possibly based on a description by Pliny.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Nama avalerion tercantum pada Peta Hereford dekat sungai Hydaspes dan Indus, mungkin berdasarkan deskripsi Plinius.",
        "en": "The term avalerion appears on the Hereford Map near the Hydaspes and the Indus, possibly from a description by Pliny."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c05",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "An example is the arms of the Duchy of Lorraine (or, on a bend gules, 3 alerions abaisé argent). It supposedly had been inspired by the assumed arms of crusader Geoffrey de Bouillon, according to a tale that he killed three white eaglets with a bow and arrow when out hunting.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Contohnya lambang Kadipaten Lorraine dengan tiga alerion perak, yang konon terilhami kisah tentara salib Godefroy dari Bouillon yang memanah tiga anak elang putih saat berburu.",
        "en": "An example is the arms of the Duchy of Lorraine with three silver alerions, said to be inspired by a tale that the crusader Godfrey of Bouillon shot three white eaglets while hunting."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c06",
      "source_id": "alerion-s1",
      "quote": "The word's ultimate origin is unclear, possibly adapted from the German Adler or Adelar (\"eagle\"). It is found in 12th-century French as alérion and in medieval Latin as alariōnem (a large eagle-like bird).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal katanya tidak jelas, mungkin dari bahasa Jerman Adler atau Adelar, \"elang\"; dalam bahasa Prancis abad ke-12 alérion dan Latin abad pertengahan alariōnem.",
        "en": "The word's origin is unclear, perhaps from German Adler or Adelar, 'eagle'; it appears in 12th-century French as alérion and in medieval Latin as alariōnem."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c07",
      "source_id": "alerion-s2",
      "quote": "Alerion atau avalerion adalah burung mitologis. Menurut geografer dan ahli hewan buas abad Pertengahan, wujudnya \"agak kecil, tetapi lebih besar daripada elang\" dan hidup di dekat Hydaspes dan Indus",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Indonesia juga menyebut alerion burung mitologis yang menurut ahli geografi dan bestiari abad pertengahan sedikit lebih besar dari elang dan hidup dekat Hydaspes dan Indus.",
        "en": "The Indonesian article also calls the alerion a mythological bird that medieval geographers and bestiarists placed near the Hydaspes and Indus, somewhat larger than an eagle."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "alerion-c08",
      "source_id": "alerion-s3",
      "quote": "Als Wappen Lothringens erscheint sie seit dem 13. Jahrhundert häufig zu dritt auf rotem Schrägbalken.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, alerion sejak abad ke-13 sering muncul bertiga pada pita miring merah dalam lambang Lorraine.",
        "en": "According to the German article, since the 13th century the alerion has often appeared in threes on a red bend in the arms of Lorraine."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {

}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Amphiptere adalah ular bersayap dalam heraldik Eropa, berbulu terang seperti matahari terbit, yang dalam legenda Wales dan Irlandia dianggap pembawa keberuntungan.",
    "en": "The amphiptere is a winged serpent of European heraldry with sunrise-bright feathers, considered a bringer of luck in legends from Wales and Ireland.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "amphiptere-c01",
      "amphiptere-c02",
      "amphiptere-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Amphiptere atau amphithere, \"bersayap dua\", adalah ular bersayap dalam heraldik Eropa, bertubuh seperti lindworm, berbulu terang seperti matahari terbit, bersayap burung atau kelelawar, dan berekor panjang seperti wyvern. Ia jarang dipakai dalam lambang, antara lain oleh Wangsa Potier dan Duke of Tresmes.",
      "en": "The amphiptere or amphithere, 'two-winged', is a winged serpent of European heraldry with a lindworm-like body, feathers bright as sunrise, bird or bat wings and a long wyvern-like tail. It was rarely used in heraldry, among others by the House of Potier and the Duke of Tresmes.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "amphiptere-c01",
        "amphiptere-c02",
        "amphiptere-c03",
        "amphiptere-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Legenda amphitere di Wales dan Irlandia menganggapnya pembawa keberuntungan, berbeda dari lindworm yang merusak. Ia mirip guivre Prancis dan dilukis pada lambang untuk menakuti musuh.",
      "en": "Legends from Wales and Ireland make the amphitere a bringer of luck, unlike the destructive lindworm. It resembles the French guivre and was painted on arms to frighten enemies.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "amphiptere-c05",
        "amphiptere-c06"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c01",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s1",
      "quote": "Amphiptere (also called Amphithere, Amphitere, or Phipthere; meaning bi-winged, two-winged) is a type of winged serpent found in European heraldry.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amphiptere (juga Amphithere, Amphitere, atau Phipthere, \"bersayap dua\") adalah sejenis ular bersayap dalam heraldik Eropa.",
        "en": "The amphiptere (also amphithere, amphitere or phipthere, 'two-winged') is a kind of winged serpent found in European heraldry."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c02",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s1",
      "quote": "Amphipteres generally were said to have light-colored feathers like a sunrise, a serpentine body similar to a lindworm, bat-like wings with feathers covering most of the forearm and often greenish in coloration, and a long tail much like a wyvern's tail.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amphiptere umumnya digambarkan berbulu terang seperti matahari terbit, bertubuh ular mirip lindworm, bersayap kelelawar yang sebagian besar berbulu dan sering kehijauan, serta berekor panjang seperti wyvern.",
        "en": "Amphipteres were generally said to have light feathers like a sunrise, a serpentine lindworm-like body, bat-like wings mostly feathered and often greenish, and a long wyvern-like tail."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c03",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s1",
      "quote": "Amphipteres saw infrequent use as heraldic devices. Amphipteres are present on the arms of the House of Potier, which depict a bendlet purpure between two amphipteres. The Potier heraldry also uses amphipteres as supporters, as do those of the Duke of Tresmes and Duke of Gesvres.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Amphiptere jarang dipakai dalam lambang; ia ada pada lambang Wangsa Potier dan sebagai penyangga lambang Duke of Tresmes dan Duke of Gesvres.",
        "en": "Amphipteres were seldom used in heraldry; they appear on the arms of the House of Potier and as supporters of the Duke of Tresmes and Duke of Gesvres."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c04",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s2",
      "quote": "In der Heraldik ist die Amphitere eine Schlange mit Vogelflügeln. Später wird sie auch mit Fledermausflügeln dargestellt.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, dalam heraldik amphitere adalah ular bersayap burung yang kemudian juga digambarkan bersayap kelelawar.",
        "en": "According to the German article, in heraldry the amphitere is a snake with bird wings, later also shown with bat wings."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c05",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s2",
      "quote": "Legenden von Amphiteren sind auch aus Wales und Irland bekannt. In den Erzählungen gelten die Fabelwesen oft als Glücksbringer, anders als die ähnlichen Lindwürmer, die destruktiv sind.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Legenda amphitere juga dikenal di Wales dan Irlandia, tempat makhluk ini sering dianggap pembawa keberuntungan, berbeda dari lindworm yang merusak.",
        "en": "Amphitere legends are also known from Wales and Ireland, where they are often bringers of luck, unlike the destructive lindworms."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "amphiptere-c06",
      "source_id": "amphiptere-s3",
      "quote": "アンピプテラは翼の生えた蛇として表現され、フランスのギーヴル（ヴイーヴル）に似ている。アンピテプラやヴイーブルといった怪物は、戦場で敵に脅威を与え威嚇する意味もあって紋章にしばしば描かれていた。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, amphiptere digambarkan sebagai ular bersayap mirip guivre (vouivre) Prancis, dan monster semacam ini sering dilukis pada lambang untuk menakuti musuh di medan perang.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, the amphiptere is shown as a winged snake like the French guivre (vouivre), and such monsters were often painted on arms to threaten enemies in battle."
      }
    }
  ],

    "id": "Biscione adalah ular ilahi dengan seorang anak di mulutnya, lambang heraldik keluarga Visconti dan kota Milan yang kini muncul pada logo Alfa Romeo.",
    "en": "The biscione is a divine serpent with a child in its mouth, the heraldic emblem of the Visconti and of Milan, now on the Alfa Romeo logo.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "biscione-c01",
      "biscione-c02",
      "biscione-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Biscione, \"ular rumput besar\" atau vipera, adalah gambar heraldik ular ilahi dengan seorang anak di mulutnya, yang ditafsirkan sedang menelan atau memuntahkan anak itu. Ia lambang Milan, semula milik keluarga Visconti lalu Sforza; salah satu gambaran tertuanya adalah fresko akhir abad ke-13 di Kastel Visconti di Angera.",
      "en": "The biscione, 'big grass snake' or vipera, is a heraldic image of a divine serpent with a child in its mouth, read as swallowing or disgorging it. It is the emblem of Milan, first of the Visconti and then the Sforza; one of its oldest depictions is a late 13th-century fresco in the Visconti Castle at Angera.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "biscione-c01",
        "biscione-c02",
        "biscione-c03",
        "biscione-c05"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Asal-usulnya mungkin ular perunggu yang dibawa dari Konstantinopel oleh Uskup Agung Arnolf II, atau menurut tradisi yang dicatat Galvano Fiamma, lambang seorang Saracen yang dikalahkan Ottone Visconti dalam duel. Kini biscione ada pada logo Alfa Romeo dan lambang beberapa kota di luar Italia seperti Dachau dan Sanok.",
      "en": "It may derive from a bronze serpent brought from Constantinople by Archbishop Arnolf II, or, by a tradition recorded by Galvano Fiamma, from a Saracen's emblem won by Ottone Visconti in a duel. Today it appears on the Alfa Romeo logo and in the arms of towns abroad such as Dachau and Sanok.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "biscione-c04",
        "biscione-c06",
        "biscione-c07",
        "biscione-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "title": "Biscione",
      "author": null,

      "published": null,

    }
  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "biscione-c01",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "The biscione (English: \"big grass snake\"), less commonly known also as the vipera, is in heraldry a charge consisting of a divine serpent with a child in its mouth; the serpent may be variously described as being in the act of swallowing the child, or the child may be emerging from its mouth.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biscione (\"ular rumput besar\"), juga disebut vipera, dalam heraldik adalah gambar ular ilahi dengan seorang anak di mulutnya, yang ditafsirkan sedang menelan anak itu atau anak itu keluar dari mulutnya.",
        "en": "The biscione ('big grass snake'), also called vipera, is a heraldic charge of a divine serpent with a child in its mouth, read either as swallowing the child or as the child emerging from its mouth."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c02",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "It is a historic symbol of the city of Milan, originally on the coat-of-arms of the Visconti and Sforza dynasties.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia lambang bersejarah kota Milan, semula pada lambang dinasti Visconti dan Sforza.",
        "en": "It is a historic symbol of Milan, originally on the arms of the Visconti and Sforza dynasties."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c03",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "Etymologically, word biscione is a masculine augmentative of Italian feminine biscia \"grass snake\" (corrupted from bistia, ultimately from Latin bestia \"beast\").",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kata biscione adalah bentuk pembesar dari biscia, \"ular rumput\", dari bistia dan akhirnya bahasa Latin bestia, \"binatang buas\".",
        "en": "Biscione is the augmentative of Italian biscia, 'grass snake', from bistia and ultimately Latin bestia, 'beast'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c04",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "The symbol may have been derived from a bronzed serpent brought to Milan from Constantinople by Arnolf II of Arsago (Archbishop of Milan 998–1018) in the 11th century.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Lambang ini mungkin berasal dari ular perunggu yang dibawa ke Milan dari Konstantinopel oleh Arnolf II dari Arsago (Uskup Agung Milan 998–1018) pada abad ke-11.",
        "en": "The symbol may derive from a bronze serpent brought to Milan from Constantinople by Arnolf II of Arsago (Archbishop of Milan 998–1018) in the 11th century."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c05",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "One of the oldest depictions of the Biscione is in the Great Hall of the Visconti Castle of Angera. The hall was painted at the end of the 13th century with frescoes celebrating Archbishop Ottone Visconti's victory against the rival family of the Della Torre.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Salah satu gambaran tertua biscione ada di Aula Besar Kastel Visconti di Angera, yang dilukis fresko pada akhir abad ke-13 untuk merayakan kemenangan Uskup Agung Ottone Visconti atas keluarga Della Torre.",
        "en": "One of the oldest depictions of the biscione is in the Great Hall of the Visconti Castle at Angera, frescoed in the late 13th century to celebrate Archbishop Ottone Visconti's victory over the Della Torre."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c06",
      "source_id": "biscione-s1",
      "quote": "The logo of automobile manufacturer Alfa Romeo (also known as the Casa del Biscione, Italian for \"House of the Biscione\" or \"Biscione['s] marque\"), established in Milan in 1910, features the flag of Milan and the biscione as the city's symbol.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Logo produsen mobil Alfa Romeo, yang didirikan di Milan pada 1910 dan dijuluki Casa del Biscione, memuat bendera Milan dan biscione.",
        "en": "The logo of the car maker Alfa Romeo, founded in Milan in 1910 and nicknamed the Casa del Biscione, shows the flag of Milan and the biscione."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c07",
      "source_id": "biscione-s2",
      "quote": "Neben zahlreichen italienischen Gemeinden findet sich der Biscione auch in den Stadtwappen von Dachau, Pružany, Dłutów und Sanok.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, selain di banyak kota Italia biscione juga ada pada lambang kota Dachau, Pružany, Dłutów, dan Sanok.",
        "en": "According to the German article, besides many Italian towns the biscione appears in the arms of Dachau, Pružany, Dłutów and Sanok."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "biscione-c08",
      "source_id": "biscione-s3",
      "quote": "Según una tradición recogida por Galvano Fiamma, Ottone Visconti habría conquistado la insignia de la víbora tras vencer en duelo a un sarraceno que la llevaba como arma, representada devorando a un hombre tanto en el escudo como sobre el yelmo.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut tradisi yang dicatat Galvano Fiamma, Ottone Visconti merebut lambang ular ini setelah mengalahkan seorang Saracen dalam duel, yang membawa gambar ular melahap manusia di perisai dan helmnya.",
        "en": "By a tradition recorded by Galvano Fiamma, Ottone Visconti won the viper emblem by defeating in a duel a Saracen who bore it, devouring a man, on shield and helm."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {

        "en": "Origin of the biscione emblem"

        }
      ]

  ],
  "gaps": [

  "traits": [],
  "short_description": {
    "id": "Biarawan laut adalah makhluk laut mirip biarawan berjubah yang konon ditangkap dekat Zealand, Denmark, pada 1546 dan tercatat dalam buku sejarah alam Belon, Rondelet, dan Gesner.",
    "en": "The sea monk is a sea creature resembling a monk in his habit, supposedly caught off Zealand, Denmark, in 1546 and recorded in the natural histories of Belon, Rondelet and Gesner.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "sea-monk-c01",
      "sea-monk-c02"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Biarawan laut adalah \"ikan\" yang dari luar mirip biarawan berjubah, konon ditangkap dekat Zealand, Denmark, sekitar 1546; Christian III mengirim gambarnya kepada Kaisar Charles V, dan panjangnya dicatat 4 ell. Belon, Rondelet, dan Gesner memasukkannya ke dalam buku sejarah alam; Rondelet menggolongkannya manusia laut tetapi meragukan gambarnya.",
      "en": "The sea monk is a 'fish' outwardly like a monk in his habit, supposedly caught off Zealand, Denmark, around 1546; Christian III sent its picture to Emperor Charles V, and it was recorded as 4 ells long. Belon, Rondelet and Gesner put it in their natural histories; Rondelet classed it as a merman but doubted the pictures.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "sea-monk-c01",
        "sea-monk-c02",
        "sea-monk-c03",
        "sea-monk-c04"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Artikel bahasa Jerman menyebut biarawan laut sudah disebut Alexander Neckam sekitar 1200, dan Gesner juga mencatat makhluk serupa di Firth of Forth dan Polandia. Japetus Steenstrup menafsirkannya sebagai cumi-cumi, sedangkan tafsiran lain menyebut ikan sungut ganda atau anjing laut.",
      "en": "The German article says the sea monk was already mentioned by Alexander Neckam around 1200, and Gesner also recorded similar creatures in the Firth of Forth and Poland. Japetus Steenstrup read it as a squid, while others suggest anglerfish or seals.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "sea-monk-c05",
        "sea-monk-c06",
        "sea-monk-c07"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "cultural_context": null,

  ],
  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c01",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s1",
      "quote": "The sea monk (also monk-fish or monkfish) was a sea creature found off the eastern coast of the Danish island of Zealand in 1546. It was described as a \"fish\" that outwardly resembled a human monk in his habit.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Biarawan laut adalah makhluk laut yang ditemukan di lepas pantai timur pulau Zealand, Denmark, pada 1546, digambarkan sebagai \"ikan\" yang dari luar mirip biarawan berjubah.",
        "en": "The sea monk was a sea creature found off the east coast of the Danish island of Zealand in 1546, described as a 'fish' outwardly like a monk in his habit."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c02",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s1",
      "quote": "The creature was called \"monk fish\" (Latin: piscis monachus) by Belon (1553), and piscis monachi habitu (\"fish [wearing] the habit of a monk\") by Rondelet (1554).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Belon (1553) menyebutnya piscis monachus, \"ikan biarawan\", dan Rondelet (1554) piscis monachi habitu, \"ikan berjubah biarawan\".",
        "en": "Belon (1553) called it piscis monachus, 'monk fish', and Rondelet (1554) piscis monachi habitu, 'fish in a monk's habit'."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c03",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s1",
      "quote": "Christian III of Denmark sent an illustration of it to Charles V, Holy Roman Emperor. The creature is recorded in Vedel's Den danske Krønicke (1575) as measuring 4 ells long (15 ft; 4.6 m).",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Christian III dari Denmark mengirim gambarnya kepada Kaisar Charles V, dan Den danske Krønicke karya Vedel (1575) mencatat panjangnya 4 ell (4,6 m).",
        "en": "Christian III of Denmark sent a picture of it to Emperor Charles V, and Vedel's Den danske Krønicke (1575) records it as 4 ells (4.6 m) long."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c04",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s1",
      "quote": "Rondelet (1554) called it \"the fish with the habit of a monk (piscis monachi habitu), and classed it as a merman (homo maris). But he did not think the pictorial representations he obtained could be taken at face value",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Rondelet menggolongkannya sebagai manusia laut (homo maris), tetapi meragukan bahwa gambar yang ia terima bisa dipercaya begitu saja.",
        "en": "Rondelet classed it as a merman (homo maris) but doubted that the pictures he received could be taken at face value."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c05",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s2",
      "quote": "Erstmals erwähnt wird er um 1200 bei Alexander Neckam. In der Folge wurden verschiedene Sichtungen des Meermönches dokumentiert, unter anderem soll um 1550 ein Exemplar dem König von Dänemark zugeschickt worden sein.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, biarawan laut pertama disebut sekitar 1200 oleh Alexander Neckam, dan berbagai penampakan kemudian dicatat, termasuk spesimen yang dikirim kepada Raja Denmark sekitar 1550.",
        "en": "According to the German article, the sea monk was first mentioned around 1200 by Alexander Neckam, and later sightings were recorded, including a specimen sent to the King of Denmark around 1550."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c06",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s2",
      "quote": "Japetus Steenstrup interpretierte ihn 1855 als Tintenfisch, neuere Deutungen gehen zum Beispiel von Seeteufeln, Meerengeln oder Robben aus.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Japetus Steenstrup pada 1855 menafsirkannya sebagai cumi-cumi, sedangkan tafsiran baru mengusulkan ikan sungut ganda, hiu malaikat, atau anjing laut.",
        "en": "Japetus Steenstrup in 1855 read it as a squid, while newer readings suggest anglerfish, angelsharks or seals."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "sea-monk-c07",
      "source_id": "sea-monk-s3",
      "quote": "Éste también hizo referencia a un monstruo similar encontrado en el Fiordo de Forth, citando al historiador escocés Hector Boece, y a un avistamiento en la costa de Polonia en 1531.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, Gesner juga menyebut monster serupa di Firth of Forth menurut sejarawan Skotlandia Hector Boece, serta penampakan di pantai Polandia pada 1531.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, Gesner also cited a similar monster in the Firth of Forth from the Scottish historian Hector Boece, and a sighting off Poland in 1531."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],
  "gaps": [
    {

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Xana adalah peri air yang sangat cantik dari mitologi Asturias yang tinggal di mata air dan gua, menjanjikan harta, bernyanyi pada malam musim panas, dan menukar bayi manusia dengan anaknya.",
    "en": "The xana is a beautiful water fairy of Asturian mythology who lives in springs and caves, promises treasure, sings on summer nights and swaps human babies for her own.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "xana-c01",
      "xana-c03",
      "xana-c04",
      "xana-c06"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Xana adalah peri perempuan yang luar biasa cantik dari mitologi Asturias, tinggal di mata air, sungai, air terjun, dan gua; namanya mungkin dari nama dewi Diana. Ia pirang dan menyisir rambutnya dengan sisir emas di tepi sungai, mandi di mata air saat pagi, menjanjikan harta, dan memberi \"air cinta\" atau emas kepada yang layak. Suaranya pada malam musim panas menenangkan orang berjiwa murni tetapi membuat gila yang tidak murni.",
      "en": "The xana is an extraordinarily beautiful female fairy of Asturian mythology who lives in springs, rivers, waterfalls and caves; her name may come from the goddess Diana. Blonde, she combs her hair with a golden comb on riverbanks, bathes in her spring in the morning, promises treasure and gives 'love water' or gold to the worthy. Her voice on summer nights calms the pure of soul but maddens the impure.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "xana-c01",
        "xana-c02",
        "xana-c03",
        "xana-c04",
        "xana-c05",
        "xana-c07",
        "xana-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Karena tidak dapat menyusui, xana menukar bayi manusia dengan anaknya, xanín, yang ketahuan bila kulit telur ditumpuk di dekat perapian. Sebagian xana juga menyerang orang dan mencuri makanan.",
      "en": "Unable to nurse, xanas swap human babies for their own xaninos, who give themselves away when eggshells are stacked by the hearth. Some xanas also attack people and steal food.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "xana-c03",
        "xana-c06",
        "xana-c08"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "xana-c01",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "The xana (Asturian: /ˈʃana/or [ˈɕa.na]) is a character found in Asturian mythology. Always female, she is a creature of extraordinary beauty believed to live in fountains, rivers, waterfalls, or forested regions with pure water.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Xana adalah tokoh mitologi Asturias, selalu perempuan dan luar biasa cantik, yang dipercaya tinggal di mata air, sungai, air terjun, atau hutan berair jernih.",
        "en": "The xana is a figure of Asturian mythology, always female and extraordinarily beautiful, believed to live in springs, rivers, waterfalls or forests with pure water."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c02",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "The origin of the Asturian word xana is unclear, though some scholars see it as a derivation from the Latin name for the goddess Diana. References to where the mythological xanas lived are still common in Asturian toponyms.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "etymology",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Asal kata xana tidak jelas, tetapi sebagian sarjana menganggapnya turunan nama Latin dewi Diana, dan tempat tinggal xana masih banyak terekam dalam nama tempat di Asturias.",
        "en": "The origin of xana is unclear, though some scholars derive it from the Latin name of the goddess Diana, and the xanas' homes are still common in Asturian place names."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c03",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "The xanas promise treasures and can be disenchanted. Some xanas also attack people and steal their food. They live in fountains and caves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Xana menjanjikan harta dan dapat dibebaskan dari sihir, sebagian juga menyerang orang dan mencuri makanan, dan mereka tinggal di mata air dan gua.",
        "en": "Xanas promise treasure and can be disenchanted; some also attack people and steal food, and they live in springs and caves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c04",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "A xana can be a beneficial spirit, offering \"love water\" to travellers and rewards of gold or silver to those found worthy through some undefined judgment. Their hypnotic voices can be heard during spring and summer nights.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Xana dapat menjadi roh baik yang memberi \"air cinta\" kepada pelancong dan emas atau perak kepada yang dianggap layak, dan suaranya yang memukau terdengar pada malam musim semi dan musim panas.",
        "en": "A xana can be kindly, offering 'love water' to travellers and gold or silver to those judged worthy, and her hypnotic voice is heard on spring and summer nights."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c05",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "Those who have a pure soul and hear the song will be filled with a sense of peace and love. Those whose souls are not pure will feel they are being suffocated and may be driven insane.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Orang berjiwa murni yang mendengar nyanyiannya dipenuhi damai dan cinta, sedangkan yang tidak murni merasa tercekik dan bisa menjadi gila.",
        "en": "The pure of soul who hear her song are filled with peace and love, while the impure feel smothered and may go mad."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c06",
      "source_id": "xana-s1",
      "quote": "Xanas have children called xaninos (singular: xanín), but because they cannot take care of them—xanas cannot produce milk to feed their babies—they usually take a human baby from his cradle and put their own fairy child in instead, similar to changelings in other cultures.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Xana memiliki anak bernama xaninos, tetapi karena tidak dapat menyusui, mereka biasanya menukar bayi manusia di buaian dengan anak peri mereka, mirip changeling di budaya lain.",
        "en": "Xanas have children called xaninos, but since they cannot make milk they usually swap a human baby in its cradle for their own fairy child, like changelings elsewhere."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c07",
      "source_id": "xana-s2",
      "quote": "Die Xana erscheint als schöne junge Frau, die morgens aus einer Gebirgshöhle kommend zu ihrer Quelle wandert, um sich im eiskalten Wasser zu baden. Nach mündlicher Überlieferung aus den Picos de Europa gehen Wünsche, die in diesen Moment an ihr Ohr dringen, in Erfüllung.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, xana tampak sebagai perempuan muda cantik yang pagi-pagi keluar dari gua gunung untuk mandi di mata airnya yang dingin, dan menurut tradisi lisan Picos de Europa keinginan yang ia dengar saat itu akan terkabul.",
        "en": "According to the German article, the xana appears as a beautiful young woman who leaves her mountain cave in the morning to bathe in her ice-cold spring, and by oral tradition of the Picos de Europa wishes she hears then come true."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c08",
      "source_id": "xana-s2",
      "quote": "Nur durch das Stapeln von Eierschalen neben dem Herdfeuer verraten sich die ausgetauschten Wechselbälger selbst, weil sie dann überrascht rufen: „Vor hundert Jahren wurde ich geboren, aber so viele Eierschalen beim Feuer habe ich noch nie gesehen!“.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Anak tukaran hanya ketahuan bila kulit telur ditumpuk di dekat perapian, karena ia akan berseru heran, \"Seratus tahun lalu aku lahir, tetapi belum pernah melihat kulit telur sebanyak ini di dekat api!\"",
        "en": "A changeling betrays itself only when eggshells are stacked by the hearth, for it cries in surprise, 'I was born a hundred years ago, but I have never seen so many eggshells by the fire!'"
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "xana-c09",
      "source_id": "xana-s3",
      "quote": "Elles se présentent comme des filles jeunes et très belles, de caractère plutôt nordique, c'est-à-dire blondes et aux yeux clairs. Elles se peignent sur les rives des rivières avec des peignes d'or, attendant des voyageurs pour les séduire.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, mereka tampak sebagai gadis muda yang sangat cantik, pirang dan bermata terang, yang menyisir rambut dengan sisir emas di tepi sungai sambil menunggu pelancong untuk dirayu.",
        "en": "According to the French article, they appear as very beautiful young girls, blonde and light-eyed, combing their hair with golden combs on riverbanks while waiting to seduce travellers."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Adamastor adalah raksasa badai penjaga Tanjung Harapan dalam epos Portugal Os Lusíadas karya Camões, yang menghadang Vasco da Gama dalam perjalanannya ke India.",
    "en": "Adamastor is the storm giant guarding the Cape of Good Hope in Camões's Portuguese epic Os Lusíadas, who confronts Vasco da Gama on his way to India.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "adamastor-c01",
      "adamastor-c04",
      "adamastor-c08"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Adamastor adalah personifikasi Tanjung Harapan dalam Os Lusíadas (1572) karya Luís de Camões, lambang bahaya laut yang diatasi bangsa Portugal pada Zaman Penjelajahan. Camões menjadikannya salah satu Raksasa Yunani yang dibuang ke tanjung itu oleh dewi Doris karena mencintai putrinya, Tethis. Ia menampakkan diri kepada Vasco da Gama sebagai awan badai raksasa, meramalkan bencana pelayaran, lalu setelah menceritakan kisahnya lenyap dan membuka jalan ke India.",
      "en": "Adamastor personifies the Cape of Good Hope in Luís de Camões's Os Lusíadas (1572), a symbol of the sea's dangers overcome by the Portuguese in the Age of Discovery. Camões made him one of the Greek Giants, banished to the cape by the goddess Doris for loving her daughter Tethis. He appears to Vasco da Gama as a huge storm cloud, foretells the voyage's disasters, then tells his story and vanishes, opening the way to India.",

        "adamastor-c01",

        "adamastor-c10"
      ]

      "en": "The French article says Adamastor already appears in Claudian's Gigantomachy before Camões popularised him. In Lisbon the Miradouro de Santa Catarina is nicknamed 'Adamastor' for its statue, Fernando Pessoa wrote O Mostrengo about him, and Victor Hugo mentions him in Les Misérables.",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c01",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "Adamastor is a mythological character created by the Portuguese poet Luís de Camões in his epic poem Os Lusíadas (first printed in 1572), as a personification of the Cape of Good Hope",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Adamastor adalah tokoh mitologis ciptaan penyair Portugal Luís de Camões dalam epos Os Lusíadas (pertama dicetak 1572) sebagai personifikasi Tanjung Harapan.",
        "en": "Adamastor is a mythological character created by the Portuguese poet Luís de Camões in his epic Os Lusíadas (first printed 1572) as a personification of the Cape of Good Hope."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c02",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "symbolizing the dangers of the sea and the formidable forces of nature challenged and ultimately overcome by the Portuguese during the Age of Discovery. Adamastor manifests itself out of a storm.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia melambangkan bahaya laut dan kekuatan alam yang ditantang dan akhirnya diatasi bangsa Portugal pada Zaman Penjelajahan, dan ia muncul dari badai.",
        "en": "He symbolises the dangers of the sea and the forces of nature challenged and finally overcome by the Portuguese in the Age of Discovery, and he appears out of a storm."

      "id": "adamastor-c03",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "Camões gave his creation a backstory as one of the Giants of Greek mythology, banished to the Cape of Good Hope by sea goddess Doris for falling in love with her daughter Tethis",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Camões menjadikannya salah satu Raksasa mitologi Yunani yang dibuang ke Tanjung Harapan oleh dewi laut Doris karena jatuh cinta pada putrinya, Tethis.",
        "en": "Camões made him one of the Giants of Greek mythology, banished to the Cape of Good Hope by the sea goddess Doris for falling in love with her daughter Tethis."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c04",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "Vasco da Gama, ahead of the Portuguese expedition, confronts the creature by asking \"Who are you?\", prompting Adamastor to tell his story.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Vasco da Gama, pemimpin ekspedisi Portugal, menghadapi makhluk itu dengan bertanya \"Siapa kau?\", sehingga Adamastor menceritakan kisahnya.",
        "en": "Vasco da Gama, leading the Portuguese expedition, confronts the creature by asking 'Who are you?', and Adamastor tells his story."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c05",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "Deeply moved, the giant eventually vanishes, dispersing the clouds and calming the sea, leaving the path towards India open.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Karena terharu, raksasa itu akhirnya lenyap, mengusir awan dan menenangkan laut sehingga jalan ke India terbuka.",
        "en": "Deeply moved, the giant finally vanishes, scattering the clouds and calming the sea so that the way to India lies open."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c06",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "A popular gathering place in Lisbon is known by the name 'Adamastor' because of the large stone statue of the mythical figure which presides over the space, which is officially called the Miradouro de Santa Catarina.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Sebuah tempat berkumpul populer di Lisboa, yang resminya bernama Miradouro de Santa Catarina, dikenal sebagai \"Adamastor\" karena patung batu besar tokoh itu di sana.",
        "en": "A popular gathering place in Lisbon, officially the Miradouro de Santa Catarina, is known as 'Adamastor' for the large stone statue of the figure there."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c07",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s1",
      "quote": "The Portuguese poet Fernando Pessoa included in his 1934 book Mensagem a number of verses dedicated to Adamastor, entitled O Mostrengo (\"The Hideous Monster\")",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Penyair Portugal Fernando Pessoa memuat dalam bukunya Mensagem (1934) beberapa bait untuk Adamastor berjudul O Mostrengo (\"Monster yang Mengerikan\").",
        "en": "The Portuguese poet Fernando Pessoa included in Mensagem (1934) verses on Adamastor titled O Mostrengo ('The Hideous Monster')."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c08",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s2",
      "quote": "Er sollte sich Vasco da Gama als mächtige Gewitterwolke offenbaren, die den Zugang zum Kap der Guten Hoffnung bzw. Cabo das Tormentas (Kap der Stürme) verteidigt, hinter dem sich der Indische Ozean erstreckt",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, ia menampakkan diri kepada Vasco da Gama sebagai awan badai raksasa yang menjaga jalan ke Tanjung Harapan atau Cabo das Tormentas (Tanjung Badai), yang di baliknya terbentang Samudra Hindia.",
        "en": "According to the German article, he appeared to Vasco da Gama as a mighty storm cloud guarding the way to the Cape of Good Hope or Cabo das Tormentas (Cape of Storms), beyond which lies the Indian Ocean."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c09",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s3",
      "quote": "Adamastor ou le géant des Tempêtes est l'esprit du Cap des Orages (Cap de Bonne-Espérance), cité par Claudien dans sa Gigantomachie, puis popularisé par Luís de Camões (1524-1580) dans Les Lusiades.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, Adamastor atau raksasa Badai adalah roh Tanjung Badai yang sudah disebut Claudianus dalam Gigantomachia-nya, lalu dipopulerkan oleh Camões dalam Os Lusíadas.",
        "en": "According to the French article, Adamastor, the giant of the Storms, is the spirit of the Cape of Storms, mentioned by Claudian in his Gigantomachy and then popularised by Camões in Os Lusíadas."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c10",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s3",
      "quote": "L'esprit est censément apparu à Vasco de Gama et avait prédit les désastres qui émaillèrent le voyage vers l'Inde.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Roh itu konon menampakkan diri kepada Vasco da Gama dan meramalkan bencana-bencana yang menimpa pelayaran ke India.",
        "en": "The spirit supposedly appeared to Vasco da Gama and foretold the disasters that beset the voyage to India."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "adamastor-c11",
      "source_id": "adamastor-s3",
      "quote": "Victor Hugo le cite dans Les Misérables, livre III, dans sa description du gamin de Paris",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Victor Hugo menyebutnya dalam Les Misérables ketika menggambarkan anak jalanan Paris.",
        "en": "Victor Hugo mentions him in Les Misérables when describing the street urchin of Paris."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Asal tokoh Adamastor",
        "en": "Origin of Adamastor"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Diciptakan oleh Camões dalam Os Lusíadas.",
            "en": "Created by Camões in Os Lusíadas."

          ]
        },
        {

            "en": "Already mentioned by Claudian, then popularised by Camões."
          },
          "claim_ids": [
            "adamastor-c09"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "gaps": [
    {
      "field": "sources",
      "searched": "Dibaca artikel Wikipedia en, de, dan fr; belum ada sumber di luar Wikipedia yang dikutip untuk entri ini."

    },

      "searched": "Gambar Commons belum dicari pada putaran ini; dijadwalkan pada pemeriksaan gambar terpisah."
    }
  ]
}

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Beast of Bray Road adalah makhluk kriptid mirip serigala yang dilaporkan terlihat di sekitar Elkhorn, Wisconsin, sejak 1980-an dan dipopulerkan oleh wartawan Linda Godfrey.",
    "en": "The Beast of Bray Road is a wolf-like cryptid reported around Elkhorn, Wisconsin, since the 1980s and popularised by reporter Linda Godfrey.",

      "beast-of-bray-road-c02"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Beast of Bray Road adalah makhluk mirip serigala setinggi 1,8–2,1 m, berbulu, berkepala serigala, dan bermata merah menyala, yang dilaporkan terlihat di jalan pedesaan Bray Road dekat Elkhorn, Wisconsin. Ia dikatakan berjalan dengan empat atau dua kaki seperti manusia serigala atau Bigfoot; seorang saksi mengaku dikejar sampai mobilnya dan pintunya tergores cakar.",
      "en": "The Beast of Bray Road is a furred wolf-headed creature 1.8–2.1 m tall with glowing red eyes, reported on the rural Bray Road near Elkhorn, Wisconsin. It is said to walk on four or two legs like a werewolf or Bigfoot; one witness claimed it chased her to her car and clawed the door.",
      "claim_ids": [

        "beast-of-bray-road-c04",

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c01",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "The Beast of Bray Road is the name given to a wolf-like creature reported to have been witnessed in or near Elkhorn, Walworth County, Wisconsin.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Beast of Bray Road adalah nama untuk makhluk mirip serigala yang dilaporkan terlihat di sekitar Elkhorn, Walworth County, Wisconsin.",
        "en": "The Beast of Bray Road is the name given to a wolf-like creature reportedly seen in or near Elkhorn, Walworth County, Wisconsin."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c02",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "Named for the rural farm road on which it was first purportedly sighted, reports of the creature in the 1980s and 1990s prompted a local newspaper, the Walworth County Week, to assign reporter Linda Godfrey to cover the story.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Dinamai menurut jalan pertanian tempat ia konon pertama terlihat, laporan tentangnya pada 1980-an dan 1990-an membuat koran lokal Walworth County Week menugaskan wartawan Linda Godfrey meliputnya.",
        "en": "Named after the farm road where it was first supposedly seen, reports in the 1980s and 1990s led the local paper Walworth County Week to assign reporter Linda Godfrey to the story."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c03",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "Skeptics maintain that the creature is likely the result of misidentification of known animals, most likely gray wolves.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Kaum skeptis berpendapat makhluk ini kemungkinan hasil salah kenal terhadap hewan biasa, paling mungkin serigala abu-abu.",
        "en": "Skeptics hold that it is probably a misidentification of known animals, most likely gray wolves."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c04",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "The Beast of Bray Road is often described by alleged witnesses as being between 6 feet (1.8 m) and 7 feet (2.1 m) tall, with a humanoid style body, covered in fur or hair, and with a head resembling a wolf and large, glowing red or orange eyes.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Para saksi sering menggambarkannya setinggi 1,8–2,1 m, bertubuh mirip manusia dan berbulu, berkepala serigala, dengan mata besar merah atau jingga yang menyala.",
        "en": "Witnesses often describe it as 1.8–2.1 m tall, humanlike in body and furred, with a wolf's head and large glowing red or orange eyes."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c05",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "It is purported to have been seen moving as both a quadruped and a biped, and some reports describe it more closely to resembling a traditional werewolf or Bigfoot.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ia konon terlihat berjalan dengan empat kaki maupun dua kaki, dan sebagian laporan menggambarkannya lebih mirip manusia serigala tradisional atau Bigfoot.",
        "en": "It is said to move on four legs and on two, and some reports liken it more to a traditional werewolf or Bigfoot."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c06",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "The creature was allegedly first sighted on two consecutive nights in 1936 on the grounds of St. Coletta School for Exceptional Children in rural Jefferson, where a night watchman witnessed it digging on an Indian burial mound located on the property.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "historical-record",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini konon pertama terlihat dua malam berturut-turut pada 1936 di halaman St. Coletta School di Jefferson, ketika seorang penjaga malam melihatnya menggali gundukan kubur Indian di sana.",
        "en": "It was supposedly first seen on two nights running in 1936 on the grounds of St. Coletta School in Jefferson, where a night watchman saw it digging at an Indian burial mound."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c07",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "The witness claimed that, upon exiting her vehicle to determine what she had hit, a large wolf-like creature with red eyes chased her back into her car, leaving claw marks in the rear passenger door.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seorang saksi mengaku setelah menabrak sesuatu di Bray Road dan keluar memeriksa, makhluk besar mirip serigala bermata merah mengejarnya kembali ke mobil dan meninggalkan bekas cakar di pintu belakang.",
        "en": "One witness claimed that after hitting something on Bray Road and getting out to look, a large red-eyed wolf-like creature chased her back to the car, leaving claw marks on the rear door."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c08",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "Others have theorized that the creature may be an American black bear suffering from mange.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "scholarly-interpretation",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ada pula yang menduga makhluk itu beruang hitam Amerika yang terserang kudis.",
        "en": "Others suggest it may be an American black bear with mange."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c09",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s1",
      "quote": "The creature was featured as the primary antagonist in the 2005 film The Beast of Bray Road, a horror film produced and distributed by The Asylum.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "modern-popular-culture",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Makhluk ini menjadi tokoh antagonis utama dalam film horor The Beast of Bray Road (2005) produksi The Asylum.",
        "en": "It was the main antagonist of the 2005 horror film The Beast of Bray Road from The Asylum."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c10",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s2",
      "quote": "La même dénomination a été, depuis lors, appliquée à toute vision de créature inconnue, du sud du Wisconsin au nord de l'Illinois et aux routes menant à l'île de Vancouver en Colombie-Britannique.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Prancis, nama itu kemudian dipakai untuk setiap penampakan makhluk tak dikenal dari selatan Wisconsin sampai utara Illinois.",
        "en": "According to the French article, the name has since been applied to any sighting of an unknown creature from southern Wisconsin to northern Illinois."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "beast-of-bray-road-c11",
      "source_id": "beast-of-bray-road-s3",
      "quote": "La Bestia de Bray Road es una supuesta criatura pseudocriptida. El primer reporte de su supuesta primera aparición es reciente y fue en los años 1980",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Spanyol, ia makhluk pseudo-kriptid yang laporan penampakan pertamanya baru muncul pada 1980-an.",
        "en": "According to the Spanish article, it is a supposed pseudo-cryptid whose first reported sighting dates only to the 1980s."
      }
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Cuélebre adalah naga-ular raksasa bersayap dari mitologi Asturias dan Cantabria yang tinggal di gua, menjaga harta, dan menawan xana, serta hanya dapat dikalahkan pada malam San Juan.",
    "en": "The cuélebre is a giant winged serpent-dragon of Asturian and Cantabrian mythology that lives in caves, guards treasure and holds xanas captive, and can be beaten only on San Juan's night.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "cuelebre-c01",
      "cuelebre-c05"
    ]
  },
  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Cuélebre atau Culebre adalah naga-ular raksasa bersayap dari Asturias dan Cantabria yang tinggal di gua, hutan, dan mata air, menjaga harta, dan sering menawan anjana atau xana. Ia abadi tetapi menua: sisiknya menjadi tak tertembus, bahkan peluru pun terpental, dan pada akhirnya ia terbang ke surga Mar Cuajada di seberang laut. Ludahnya menjadi batu penyembuh.",
      "en": "The cuélebre or culebre is a giant winged serpent-dragon of Asturias and Cantabria that lives in caves, forests and springs, guards treasure and often holds anjanas or xanas captive. Immortal yet ageing, its scales become impenetrable, even bouncing bullets, and at last it flies to the paradise of Mar Cuajada beyond the sea. Its spit becomes a healing stone.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cuelebre-c01",
        "cuelebre-c02",
        "cuelebre-c04",
        "cuelebre-c09"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Ia jarang bergerak kecuali untuk memakan ternak dan manusia, dan dapat dibunuh dengan roti berisi jarum atau batu membara; penduduk Cudillero konon memberinya roti agar ternak selamat. Pada malam San Juan sihirnya melemah sehingga pahlawan dapat mengalahkannya, menikahi xana, dan mengambil hartanya, tetapi di Cantabria ia makin kuat pada malam Santo Bartolomeus. Tidak semua cuélebre ganas; ada yang merawat xana.",
      "en": "It seldom moves except to eat cattle and people, and can be killed with a loaf full of pins or a red-hot stone; Cudillero villagers reportedly fed it bread to spare their herds. On San Juan's night its spells weaken so a hero can defeat it, marry the xana and take its treasure, but in Cantabria it grows stronger on Saint Bartholomew's Night. Not every cuélebre is fierce; some look after xanas.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "cuelebre-c03",
        "cuelebre-c05",
        "cuelebre-c06",
        "cuelebre-c08",
        "cuelebre-c10",
        "cuelebre-c11"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "claims": [
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c01",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "Cuélebre (Asturian) or Culebre (Cantabrian) is a legendary creature in the mythology of Asturias and Cantabria in northern Spain. It is depicted as a giant, winged serpent-dragon that inhabits caves and guards treasures, often keeping anjanas (fairy-like beings, also known as xanas) captive.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Cuélebre (Asturias) atau Culebre (Cantabria) adalah makhluk legenda Spanyol utara berupa naga-ular raksasa bersayap yang tinggal di gua, menjaga harta, dan sering menawan anjana atau xana.",
        "en": "Cuélebre (Asturian) or Culebre (Cantabrian) is a legendary creature of northern Spain, a giant winged serpent-dragon that lives in caves, guards treasure and often holds anjanas or xanas captive."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c02",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "Over time, these creatures age, their scales become impenetrable, and bat-like wings grow on their bodies. Although immortal, they eventually leave their homeland and fly to a mythical paradise called Mar Cuajada, located beyond the sea.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Seiring waktu mereka menua, sisiknya menjadi tak tertembus, dan tumbuh sayap mirip kelelawar; meski abadi, akhirnya mereka meninggalkan tanah asalnya dan terbang ke surga mitis Mar Cuajada di seberang laut.",
        "en": "Over time they age, their scales grow impenetrable and bat-like wings grow; though immortal, they finally leave their homeland and fly to a mythical paradise, Mar Cuajada, beyond the sea."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c03",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "They do not usually move, but when they do, it is in order to eat cattle and people. To defeat the creature, one may feed it a red-hot stone or a loaf of bread filled with pins, which would cause its death.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Mereka jarang bergerak kecuali untuk memakan ternak dan manusia, dan dapat dibunuh dengan diberi makan batu membara atau roti berisi jarum.",
        "en": "They seldom move except to eat cattle and people, and can be killed by feeding them a red-hot stone or a loaf filled with pins."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c04",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "Its spit is believed to turn into a magical stone with the power to heal various diseases.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Ludahnya dipercaya berubah menjadi batu ajaib yang dapat menyembuhkan berbagai penyakit.",
        "en": "Its spit is believed to turn into a magic stone that heals many diseases."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c05",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "According to Asturian and Cantabrian folklore, Midsummer (the night of San Juan) is a magical time when brave individuals can challenge the cuélebre, as its spells lose their power. Defeating it on this night allows one to marry the xana and claim its treasure.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut cerita rakyat Asturias dan Cantabria, pada malam San Juan sihir cuélebre kehilangan kekuatan sehingga orang pemberani dapat menantangnya, dan yang mengalahkannya boleh menikahi xana dan mengambil hartanya.",
        "en": "In Asturian and Cantabrian folklore, on Midsummer night (San Juan) its spells fail so the brave can challenge it, and whoever defeats it may marry the xana and take its treasure."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c06",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s1",
      "quote": "However, in Cantabrian lore, Saint Bartholomew's Night is said to be when the cuélebre becomes even more powerful, unleashing its wrath in vengeance against humans.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Namun dalam cerita Cantabria, pada malam Santo Bartolomeus cuélebre justru makin kuat dan melampiaskan amarahnya kepada manusia.",
        "en": "But in Cantabrian lore, on Saint Bartholomew's Night the cuélebre grows stronger and vents its wrath on humans."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c07",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s2",
      "quote": "Der Cuélebre (asturisch: Cuélebre, kantabrisch: Culebre) ist ein riesiger, geflügelter, schlangenähnlicher Drache aus der asturischen und kantabrischen Mythologie.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Artikel bahasa Jerman juga menyebut cuélebre naga raksasa bersayap mirip ular dari mitologi Asturias dan Cantabria.",
        "en": "The German article also calls the cuélebre a giant winged snake-like dragon of Asturian and Cantabrian mythology."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c08",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s2",
      "quote": "Ihre erlahmten Flügel wachsen mit der Zeit in den Körper. Meistens liegen sie regungslos herum und bewegen sich nur, um Rinder oder Menschen zu fressen.",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jerman, sayap mereka yang lumpuh lama-lama menyatu ke dalam tubuh, dan mereka kebanyakan berbaring diam kecuali untuk memakan ternak atau manusia.",
        "en": "According to the German article, their lamed wings gradually grow into the body, and they mostly lie motionless except to eat cattle or people."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c09",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s3",
      "quote": "この竜は主に森や地下洞窟や源泉に住んでいるという。その体は、弾丸すらはじき飛ばすほど堅い鱗に覆われ、飛ぶことのできる羽を持つ。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Menurut artikel bahasa Jepang, naga ini terutama tinggal di hutan, gua bawah tanah, dan mata air, bersisik sekeras sehingga peluru pun terpental, dan bersayap yang dapat terbang.",
        "en": "According to the Japanese article, the dragon lives mainly in forests, underground caves and springs, its scales so hard that even bullets bounce off, with wings it can fly with."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c10",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s3",
      "quote": "アストゥリアス州クディリェーロ ... ブラーニャセカ洞窟 (Brañaseca) のクエレブレには、家畜を食べられないように村人がとうもろこしやライ麦のパンを与えていたという。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Untuk cuélebre di gua Brañaseca, Cudillero, penduduk desa konon memberi roti jagung atau gandum hitam agar ternak mereka tidak dimakan.",
        "en": "For the cuélebre of the Brañaseca cave in Cudillero, villagers reportedly fed it corn or rye bread so it would not eat their livestock."
      }
    },
    {
      "id": "cuelebre-c11",
      "source_id": "cuelebre-s3",
      "quote": "強暴なクエレブレばかりとは限らない。金髪をもつ水の妖精・シャナの面倒をみている竜もいるという。",
      "locator": null,
      "context": "traditional-belief",
      "statement": {
        "id": "Tidak semua cuélebre ganas; ada yang konon merawat xana, peri air berambut pirang.",
        "en": "Not every cuélebre is fierce; some are said to look after the xana, the blonde water fairy."
      }
    }
  ],
  "conflicts": [
    {
      "topic": {
        "id": "Sayap cuélebre",
        "en": "The cuélebre's wings"
      },
      "positions": [
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Sayap kelelawar tumbuh seiring usia dan ia dapat terbang.",
            "en": "Bat-like wings grow with age and it can fly."
          },
          "claim_ids": [

          ]
        },
        {
          "summary": {
            "id": "Sayapnya lumpuh dan menyatu ke tubuh.",
            "en": "Its lamed wings grow into its body."
          },
          "claim_ids": [

          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  ],

  "short_description": {
    "id": "Peluda atau La Velue adalah naga berbulu hijau dengan duri beracun yang konon meneror tepi Sungai Huisne dan La Ferté-Bernard, Prancis, pada Abad Pertengahan.",
    "en": "The Peluda or La Velue is a green-furred dragon with poison spines said to have terrorised the River Huisne and La Ferté-Bernard, France, in the Middle Ages.",
    "claim_ids": [
      "peluda-c01",
      "peluda-c02"

  "long_description": [
    {
      "id": "Peluda atau La Velue, \"Yang Berbulu\", adalah binatang mitis sebesar lembu dengan kepala dan ekor ular dan tubuh bulat telur berbulu hijau panjang dengan duri beracun. Konon ia ditolak masuk Bahtera Nuh tetapi selamat dari Air Bah, lalu tinggal di Sungai Huisne dan meneror La Ferté-Bernard: ia menimbulkan banjir atau menyemburkan api yang merusak panen, memakan ternak dan manusia, dan membunuh dengan ekornya.",
      "en": "The Peluda or La Velue, 'The Hairy One', was an ox-sized beast with serpent head and tail and an egg-shaped body of long green fur with poison spines. Refused a place on Noah's Ark, it survived the Flood and lived in the River Huisne, terrorising La Ferté-Bernard: it caused floods or spat fire that ruined crops, ate livestock and people, and killed with its tail.",
      "claim_ids": [
        "peluda-c01",
        "peluda-c02",
        "peluda-c06",
        "peluda-c08"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "Setelah mencoba memangsa gadis l'Agnelle, tunangannya membunuhnya dengan menebas ekornya, satu-satunya titik lemahnya, di jembatan Yvré-l'Évêque, dan kemenangan itu lama dirayakan. Nama \"Peluda\" dipopulerkan Borges pada 1957; Cordonnier-Détrie menganggapnya sekeluarga dengan Tarasque, dan sejak 2007 ada air mancur velue di Tuffé.",
      "en": "After it tried to prey on the maiden l'Agnelle, her fiancé killed it with a stroke to the tail, its only weak spot, at the bridge of Yvré-l'Évêque, and the victory was long celebrated.
